ardhena jauhavasyāgniṃ yajati | ardhena somam 11
aupabhṛtaṃ juhvāmānīya viṣṇumiṣṭvā pratyākramya yā te agne'yāśayā tanūriti sruveṇopasadaṃ juhoti 12
agnīnmadantyāpā3

supūrvāhne paurvāhṇikībhyāṃ pravargyopasadbhyāṃ caranti | svaparāhṇa āparāhṇikībhyām 1
savyottānaistu sāyaṃ nihnavaḥ 2
eṣā prathamā 3
evamuttarābhyāṃ caryā 4
rajāśayāṃ dvitīyāyāṃ juhoti | harāśayāṃ tṛtīyāyām 5
yāḥ prātaryājyā ityuktam 6
tisra eva sāhnasyopasadaḥ | dvādaśāhīnasya 7
yadi saṃgrāmaṃ puro vā yudhyeyurayaḥ prathamāyāmavadhāya juhuyāt | rajataṃ dvitīyāyām | haritamuttamāyām 8
ārāgrāmiti stanakalpaḥ 9
yadahaḥ somaṃ krīṇīyuścaturaḥ sāyaṃ duhyustrīnprātardvau sāyamekamuttame | sarvānsāyamāśire 10
antarā madhyame pravargyopasadau vedi kurvanti 11
prāgvaṃśasya madhyamāllalāṭikāttrīnprācaḥ prakramānprakramya śaṅkuṃ nihanti 12
tasmātpañcadaśasu dakṣiṇataḥ | evamuttarataḥ | te śroṇī | prathamanihatācchaṅkoḥ ṣaṭtriṃśatiḥ purastāt | tasmāddvādaśasu dakṣiṇataḥ | evamuttarataḥ | tāvaṃsau 13

iti caturthī kaṇḍikā

imāṃ naraḥ kṛṇuta vedimetya vasumatīṃ rudravatīmādityavatīṃ varṣmandivo nābhā pṛthivyā yathāyaṃ yajamāno na riṣyeddevasya savituḥ sava iti saṃpreṣyati 1
sphyena vighanena parśvā paraśunā ca vediṃ kurvanti 2
darśapūrṇamāsavatsaṃnamanavarjaṃ prāguttarātparigrāhātkṛtvā catuḥśikhaṇḍā yuvatiḥ supeśā ghṛtapratīkā bhuvanasya madhye | tasyāṃ suparṇāvadhi yau niviṣṭau tayordevānāmadhi bhāgadheyamityabhimantrya 3

prokṣya barhistrirvediṃ prokṣati 1
nāprokṣitāmabhicaranti 2

irāvatī dhenumatī iti juhoti 1
apa janyaṃ bhayaṃ nudāpa cakrāṇi vartaya | gṛhaṃ somasya gacchatamiti vitṛtīyadeśe vedyā adhvaryurhotā brahmā maitrāvaruṇo vā padāpanudati | loṣṭaṃ vā bahirvedi nirasyati 2
āhavanīyāttrīnpratīcaḥ prakramānucchiṣyātra ramethāmiti nabhyasthe sthāpayitvā vaiṣṇavamasi viṣṇustvottabhnātvityupastabhya divo vā viṣṇavityadhvaryurdakṣiṇasya havirdhānasya dakṣiṇaṃ karṇātardamanu methīṃ nihanti | tasyāmīṣāṃ ninahyati 3

viṣṇo rarāṭamasīti purastādrarāṭyāṃ tiryañcaṃ vaṃśaṃ dhārayanviṣṇoḥ syūrasīti syūtvā viṣṇordhruvamasīti prajñātaṃ granthiṃ kṛtvā prāco vaṃśānatyādhāya viṣṇoḥ
pṛṣṭhamasīti teṣu madhyamaṃ chadiradhyūhati tryaratnivistāraṃ navāyāmam 1
tūṣṇīmitare chadiṣī adhyūhya kaṭāṃstejanīriti chadyantarāleṣu pravartamupāsyati 2
te'ntarvartā bhavanti 3
pari tvā girvaṇo gira iti sarvataḥ pariśritya viṣṇoḥ
śnaptre stha iti rarāṭyā aṃtau vyasyati | saṃmṛśatītyeke 4
viṣṇoḥ syūrasītyadhvaryurdakṣiṇāṃ dvārbāhuṃ syūtvā viṣṇordhruvamasīti prajñātaṃ granthiṃ karoti 5
granthikaraṇameke pūrvaṃ samāmananti 6
yaṃ prathamaṃ granthiṃ grathnīyādityuktam 7
evamuttarāṃ mpratiprasthātā 8
evamapare sīvyataḥ 9

ityaṣṭamī kaṇḍikā iti tṛtīyaḥ paṭalaḥ

pra tadviṣṇu stavate vīryāya mṛgo na bhīmaḥ kucaro giriṣṭhāḥ | yasyoruṣu triṣu vikramaṇeṣvadhikṣiyanti bhuvanāni viśveti saṃmitātprāṅ yajamāno niṣkramyāhavanīyāttrīnprācaḥ prakramānprakrāmati yasyoruṣviti 1

akṣṇayā dveṣyasya 6

dakṣiṇasya havirdhānasyādhastātpuro'kṣaṃ catura uparavānavāntaradeśeṣu prādeśamukhānprādeśāntarālānkaroti 1
devasya tvā savituḥ prasava ityabhrimādāya parilikhitaṃ rakṣaḥ parilikhitā arātaya iti trirdakṣiṇapūrvaṃ parilikhati 2
evamitarānpradakṣiṇamuttarāpavargam 3

prathamaṃ vā sarveṣu 1
virāḍasi sapatnahā samrāḍasi bhrātṛvyahā svarāḍasyabhimātihā viśvārāḍasi viśvāsāṃ nāṣṭrāṇāṃ hanteti bāhū upāvaharato'dhvaryuryajamānaśca 2
saṃmṛśa imānāyuṣe varcase ca devānāṃ nidhirasi dveṣoyavanaḥ | yuyodhyasmaddveṣāṃsi yāni kāni ca yakṛma | devānāmidaṃ nihitaṃ yadastathābhāhi pradiśaścatasraḥ | kṛṇvāno anyām̐ adharānsapatnānityuparavānsaṃmṛśya dakṣiṇapūrvaṃ yajamāno'vamṛśati | uttarāparamadhvaryuḥ 3
atha yajamānaḥ pṛcchatyadhvaryo kimatreti | bhadramitītaraḥ pratyāha | tannau sahetyuktvottarapūrvaṃ yajamāno'vamṛśati | dakṣiṇāparamadhvaryuḥ | tathaiva praśnaḥ prativacanaṃ ca | tanma ityāha yajamānaḥ 4
rakṣohaṇo valagahanaḥ prokṣāmi vaiṣṇavāniti yava

evaṃ sarvānkaroti 6

brahmajyotirasi suvardhāmeti brahmāṇam | sadasyo'si malimluca iti sadasyam | samudro'si viśvabharā iti sadaḥ | ajo'syekapāditi śālāmukhīyam | ahirasi budhniya iti prājahitam | kavyo'si kavyavāhana iti dakṣiṇāgnim | āyurbṛhattadaśīya tanmāvatu tasya nāmnā vṛścāmi yo 'smāndveṣṭi yaṃ ca vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ | viśvāyurvāmadebyaṃ tadaśīya tanmāvatu tasya nāmnā vṛścāmi yo'smāndveṣṭi yaṃ ca vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ | āyuḥpati rathaṃtaraṃ tadaśīya tanmāvatu tasya nāmnā vṛścāmi yo'smāndveṣṭi yaṃ ca vayaṃ dviṣma ityetaiśca pratimantraṃ havirdhānāgnīdhrasadāṃsīti 1

agnīṣomīyasya paśostantraṃ prakramayati 1
tasya nirūḍhapaśubandhavatkalpaḥ 2

ā somaṃ dadate 17
iti ṣoḍaśī kaṇḍikā

ā grāvṇa ā vāyavyānyā droṇakalaśam | utpatnīmānayanti | anvanāṃsi pravartayanti yāyāvarasya yānyaparasmingārhapatye bhavanti | ajamanunayanti | idhmābarhirājyāni prokṣaṇīrityanuharanti 1
śālāmukhīye praṇayanīyamidhmamādīpya sikatābhirupayamyāgnīṣomābhyāṃ praṇīyamānābhyāmanubrūhīti saṃpreṣyati | praṇīyamānābhyāmanubrūhīti vā 2
prathamāyāṃ triranūktāyāmayaṃ no agnirvarivaḥ kṛṇotvityagniprathamāḥ somaprathamā vā prāñco'bhipravrajanti 3
āgnīdhrīye'gniṃ pratiṣṭhāpyāgne nayetyardhamājyaśeṣasya juhoti 4
grāvṇo vāyavyāni droṇakalaśamāgnīdhra upavāsayati 5
uttareṇāgnīdhrīyamāhavanīyaṃ gatvoru viṣṇo vikramasveti sarvamājyaśeṣaṃ juhoti 6
hute'mātyāḥ pradakṣiṇamāvṛtya yathetamupāvartante 7
somā jigāti gātuvidityaparayā dvārā havirdhānaṃ rājānaṃ prapādayati | pūrvayā gataśriyaḥ 8
pūrvayā yajamānaḥ prapadyate 9
dakṣiṇasya havirdhānasya nīḍe pūrvavatkṛṣṇājināstaraṇaṃ rājñaścāsādanam 10
iti saptadaśī kaṇḍikā

athainaṃ yajamāno devatābhyaḥ saṃprayacchatyeṣa vo deva savitaḥ soma iti 1
etattvaṃ soma devo devānupāgā ityabhimantryedamahaṃ manuṣyo manuṣyāniti pradakṣiṇamāvṛtya namo devebhya iti prācīnamañjaliṃ kṛtvā svadhā pitṛbhya iti dakṣiṇedamahaṃ nirvaruṇasya pāśādityupaniṣkramya svarabhivyakhyamiti prāṅ prekṣate | suvarabhivikhyeṣamiti sarvaṃ vihāramanuvīkṣate | vaiśvānaraṃ jyotirityāhavanīyam 2
atra yajamāno'vāntaradīkṣāṃ visṛjate 3
agne vratapate tvaṃ vratānāṃ vratapatirasītyāhavanīyamupatiṣṭhate | etenaivāsminsamidhamādadhātīti vāja
saneyakam 4
vitarāṃ mekhalāṃ visraṃsate | vitarāṃ muṣṭī karṣate 5
atra daṇḍapradānameke samāmananti 6
svāhā yajñaṃ manasā svāhā dyāvāpṛthivībhyāṃ svāhororantarikṣātsvāhā yajñaṃ vātādārabha iti muṣṭī visṛjate 7
svāhā vā vivāte visṛja iti vācam 8
nivartate vratam 9
somānhaviḥśeṣāniti sutye'hani bhakṣayati 10
uttareṇāhavanīyaṃ prāgagramidhmābarhirupasādayati |
dakṣiṇamidhmamuttaraṃ barhiḥ 11
ityaṣṭādaśī kaṇḍikā

prokṣaṇīnāmabhimantraṇādi karma pratipadyate 1
staraṇakāle'pareṇottaravediṃ barhiḥ stṛṇāti 2
staraṇamantro'bhyāvartate 3
ājyānāṃ sādanādi pāśukaṃ karma pratipadyate samānamā pravarāt 4
daivaṃ ca mānuṣaṃ ca hotārau vṛtvāśrāvamāśrāvamṛtupraiṣādibhiḥ saumikānṛtvijo vṛṇīte 5
apisṛjya tṛṇamasphya uttarān 7
agnimāgnīdhrādityāgnīdhram | aśvinādhvaryū ādhvaryavādityadhvaryū | mitrāvaruṇau praśāstārau praśāstrāditi maitrāvaruṇam | indro brahmā brāhmaṇāditi brāhmaṇācchaṃsinam | marutaḥ potrāditi potāram | gnāvo neṣṭrāditi neṣṭāram | agnirdaivīnāṃ viśāṃ puraetāyaṃ yajamāno manuṣyāṇāṃ tayornāvasthūri gārhapatyaṃ dīdayacchataṃ himā dvā yū rādhāṃsītsaṃpṛñcānāvasaṃpṛñcānau tanva iti
yajamānam 8
ayaṃ sunvanyajamāno manuṣyāṇāmiti vā 9
savanīye varaṇameke samāmananti | tatra sunvanniti brūyāt 10
sarvatropāṃśu nāmagrahaṇam | mānuṣa ityuccaiḥ 11
ityekonaviṃśī kaṇḍikā iti saptamaḥ paṭalaḥ

pravṛtaḥpravṛtaḥ pravṛtahomau juhoti juṣṭo vāco bhūyāsamṛcā stomaṃ samardhayetyetābhyām 1
divā prayājaiḥ pracaryāstaṃyantamanūyājairupāsate 2
hutāyāṃ vapāyāṃ mārjayitvā subrahmaṇya pitāputrīyāṃ subrahmaṇyāmāhvayeti saṃpreṣyati 3
pitāputrīyaivāta ūrdhvaṃ subrahmaṇyā bhavati 4
āhūtāyāṃ vasatīvarīḥ kumbhena giribhidāṃ vahantīnāṃ pratyaṅtiṣṭhangṛhṇāti 5
nāntamā vahantīratīyāt 6
chāyāyai cātapataśca saṃdhau gṛhṇāti 7
yadyabhicchāyāṃ na vindedātmano vṛkṣasya kūlasya vā chāyāyām 8
pratīpamupamārayanhaviṣmatīrimā āpa iti gṛhṇāti 9
yasyāgṛhītā abhinimrocetsuvarna gharmaḥ svāheti pañcārkāhutīrhutvā vare datta ulkāmupariṣṭāddhārayamāṇo gṛhṇīyāt | hiraṇyaṃ vāvadhāya 10
yo vā brāhmaṇo bahuyājī tasya kumbhyānāṃ gṛhṇīyāt 11
somayājī bahuyājī bhavatīti vijñāyate 12
agnervo'pannagṛhasya sadasi sādayāmītyapareṇa śālāmukhīyamupasādayati | sumnāya sumninīḥ sumne mā dhatteti sarveṣu vasatīvarīṇāṃ sādaneṣu yajamāno japati | agnīṣomīyasya paśupuroḍāśasya pātrasaṃsādanādi karma pratipadyate 13
na yajamāno'gnīṣomīyasyāśnāti | aśnīyādvā 14
na svaruṃ juhoti | na hṛdayaśūlamudvāsayati | evaṃ savanīye 15
patnīsaṃyājānto'gnīṣomīyaḥ saṃtiṣṭhate 16
iti viṃśī kaṇḍikā

niśāyāṃ vasatīvarīḥ pariharatyantarvedyāsīne yajamāne patnyāṃ ca 1
nādīkṣitamabhipariharet 2
savye'ṃse'tyādhāyāpareṇa prājahitaṃ parikramya pūrvayā dvāropanirhṛtya dakṣiṇena vediṃ gatvā dakṣiṇena mārjālīyaṃ dhiṣṇiyaṃ parītya dakṣiṇasyāmuttaravediśroṇyāṃ sādayatīndrāgniyorbhāgadheyī stheti 3
dakṣiṇe'ṃse 'tyādhāya yathetaṃ gatvā pūrvayā dvāropanirhṛtyottareṇa vediṃ gatvottareṇāgnīdhrīyaṃ dhiṣṇiyaṃ parītyottarasyāmuttaravediśroṇyāṃ sādayati mitrāvaruṇayorbhāgadheyī stheti 4
savye'ṃse tyādhāya yathetaṃ gatvāpareṇāgnīdhrīyaṃ dhiṣṇiyamupasādayati viśveṣāṃ devānāṃ bhāgadheyī stheti 5
yajñe jāgṛteti sannā abhimantrayate 6
atra pratiprasthātā payasyārthaṃ sāyaṃdohaṃ dohayati 7
adhvaryuḥ saṃprreṣyati yā yajamānasya vratadhuktasyā āśiraṃ kuruta yā patniyai tasyai dadhigrahāya yā gharmadhuktasyai dadhigharmāya taptamanātaktaṃ maitrāvaruṇāya śṛtātaṅkyaṃ dadhi kurutādityagrahāya subrahmaṇya subrahmaṇyāmāhvaya na sadasyupavastavā iti 8
saṃpraiṣavatkurvanti 9
atra savanīyasya savanīyānāmitīdhmābarhiḥ saṃnahyati 10
na vā barhiḥ | prastarameva 11
āgnīdhre havirdhāne vā yajamānaṃ jāgarayanti | prāgvaṃśe patnīm 12
āgnīdhra etāṃ rātrimṛtvijo vasanti 13

ityekaviṃśī kaṇḍikā ityaṣṭamaḥ paṭalaḥ
ityekādaśaḥ praśnaḥ

mahārātre buddhvāgne nayetyāgnīdhramabhimṛśati 1
idaṃ viṣṇurvicakrama iti havirdhānam | agna āyūṃṣi pavasa iti srucaḥ | ā vāyo bhūṣa śucipā iti vāyavyāni | ā ghā ye agnimindhata iti sadaḥ 2
prajāpatirmanasāndho 'ccheta iti trayastriṃśatamāgnīdhre yajñatanūrjuhoti | prathamena mantreṇa hutvā pūrvaṃpūrvamanudrutyottareṇottareṇa juhoti 3
prādeśamātrāṇyūrdhvasānūnyupariṣṭādāsecanavanti madhye saṃnatāni vāyavyāni bhavanti 4
teṣāṃ yānyanādiṣṭavṛkṣāṇi vaikaṅkatāni syuḥ | yo vā yajñiyo vṛkṣaḥ phalagrahiḥ 5
ko vo yunakti sa vo yunaktviti khare pātrāṇi prayunakti yānyanāmnātamantrāṇi bhavanti 6
agnirdevateti dakṣiṇe'ṃse upāṃśupātram 7
somo devatetyuttaramantaryāmasya 8
bṛhannasīti te antareṇa grāvāṇamupāṃśusavanaṃ dakṣiṇāmukhaṃ saṃspṛṣṭaṃ pātrābhyām 9
tamapareṇa pratyañci dvidevatyapātrāṇi 10
indro devateti parisragaindravāyavasya | bṛhaspatirdevatetyajagāvaṃ maitrāvaruṇasya | aśvinau devateti dvisraktyāśvinasya 11
tānyapareṇa prabāhukśukrāmantinoḥ pātre | sūryo devateti dakṣiṇaṃ bailvaṃ śukrasya | candramā devatetyuttaraṃ vaikaṅkataṃ manthinaḥ 12
te apareṇa prabāhugṛtupātre āśvatthe aśvaśaphabudhne ubhayatomukhe | dakṣiṇamadhvaryoḥ | uttaram pratiprasthātuḥ 13
viśve devā devateti dakṣiṇasyāṃ śroṇyāmāgrayaṇasthālīm | indro devatetyuttarasyāmukthyasthālīm | ukthyapātraṃ ca tasyā uttaram 14
sthālyāvantareṇa trīṇyudañcyatigrāhyapātrāṇi | āgneyamaindraṃ sauryamiti 15
iti prathamā kaṇḍikā

uttare'ṃse dadhigrahapātramaudumbaraṃ catuḥsrakti 1
evarupamevāṃśvadābhyayoḥ 2
yadi somagrahaṃ gṛhṇīyādetadeva vibhavet 3
etasyaiva havirdhānasyāgreṇopastambhanamādityasthālīm | ādityapātraṃ ca tasyā uttaram 4
pṛthivī devatetyuttarasya havirdhānasyāgreṇopastambhanamanupopte dhruvasthālīm 5
khare ṣoḍaśipātraṃ khādiraṃ catuḥsrakti yadi ṣoḍaśī 6
madhye pariplavāṃ yathā srugadaṇḍaivam 7
yathāvakāśaṃ daśa camasānnaiyyagrodhānrauhītakānvā tsarumato'tsarukānvā 8
daśaiva camasādhvaryavaḥ 9
yunajmi te pṛthivīṃ jyotiṣā saheti dakṣiṇasya havirdhānasyādhastātpaścādakṣaṃ droṇakalaśaṃ sadaśāpavitram 10
tasya vāyavyairvṛkṣaniyogaḥ 11
yunajmi vāyumantarikṣeṇa te sahetyuttarasya havirdhānasyopariṣṭānnīḍa ādhavanīyam | yunajmi vācaṃ saha sūryeṇa ta iti pradhura pūtabhṛtam 12
etasyaiva havirdhānasyādhastātpaścādakṣaṃ trīnekadhanānghaṭān | pañca sapta navaikādaśa vā 13
yasminmimīte tasyādhiṣavaṇacarma kharaṃ parikṛttaṃ catuṣpuṭamupariṣṭādāsecanavat 14
rakṣohaṇo valagahanaḥ prokṣāmi vaiṣṇavamityadhiṣavaṇacarma prokṣya rakṣoghno valagaghnaḥ prokṣāmi vaiṣṇavāniti grāvṇo rakṣohā tvā valagahā vaiṣṇavamāstṛṇāmītyadhiṣavaṇaphalakayoruttaralomāstīrya rakṣoghno vo valagaghnaḥ saṃsādayāmi vaiṣṇavāniti tasmiṃścaturo grāvṇaḥ prādeśamātrānūrdhvasānūnāhananaprakārānaśmanaḥ saṃsādayati | uparaṃ prathiṣṭhaṃ madhye pañcamam 15
tamabhisaṃmukhā bhavanti 16
iti dvitīyā kaṇḍikā

sthavīyāṃsi mukhāni 1
apāṃ kṣayā ṛtasya garbhā bhuvanasya gopāḥ śyenā atithayaḥ parvatānāṃ kakubhaḥ prayuto na pātāraḥ | vagnunendraṃ hvayata ghoṣeṇāmīvāṃścātayata | yuktā stha vahata svargaṃ lokaṃ yajamānamabhivahateti sannānabhimantryāgnīṣomīyavatsavanīyapātrāṇi prayunakti | agnīṣomīyavadājyāni gṛhṇāti 2
athaikeṣām | prāgvaṃśe'gnīṣomīyasyājyāni gṛhṇāti | āgnīdhre savanīyasya | uttaravedyāmanūbandhyāyāḥ 3
apareṇottaravediṃ savanīyasyānūbandhyāyāścājyāni gṛhṇātītyeke 4
yāni kāṣṭhāni tadaharabhyādhāsyansyāttāni sahedhmena prokṣet 5

atra saumikānāṃ pātrāṇāṃ saṃsādanameke samāmananti 8

pañcahotāraṃ cāgnīdhre svargakāmasya 10
nityavadeke samāmananti 11
madhye'gnerājyāhutīḥ paśvāhutīḥ puroḍāśāhutīriti juhoti | abhitaḥ somāhutīḥ 12
atra rājānamantareṣe grāvasūpāvaharati hṛde tvā soma rājannityetābhyām 13
purā vācaḥ purā vā vayobhyaḥ pravaditoḥ prātaranuvākamupākaroti 14
prātaryāvabhyo devebhyo'nubrūhi brahmanvācaṃ yaccha pratiprasthātaḥ savanīyānnirvapa subrahmaṇya subrahmaṇyāmāhvayeti
saṃpreṣyati 15

dve bharjanārthe kapāle aṣṭau puroḍāśakapālāni | ekādaśa mādhyaṃdine | dvādaśa tṛtīyasavane 1
sarvānaindrānekādaśakapālānanusavanameke samāmananti 2
prātardohapātrāṇīti 3

indrāya harivate dhānā indrāya pūṣaṇvate karambhaṃ sarasvatyai bhāratyai parivāpamindrāya puroḍāśaṃ mitrāvaruṇābhyāṃ payasyāmiti 6
nirupteṣvanvopyedaṃ devānāmityetadādi karma pratipadyate 7

yatrābhijānātyabhūduṣā ruśatpaśuriti tatpracaraṇyā juhoti śṛṇotvagniḥ samidhā havaṃ ma iti 1
aparaṃ caturgṛhītaṃ gṛhītvā saṃpreṣyatyapa iṣya hotarmaitrāvaruṇasya camasādhvaryavādravaikadhanina ādravata neṣṭaḥ patnīmudānayonnetarhotṛcamasena vasatīvarībhiśca cātvālaṃ pratyāsveti 2
prehyudehīti neṣṭā patnīmudānayati | ehyudehīti vā | pānnejanīṃ sthālīṃ dhārayamāṇam 3
tīrthenābhipravrajanti 4
yatra hotuḥ prātaranuvākamanubruvata upaśṛṇuyustadapo'dhvaryurvahantīnāṃ gṛhṇāti 5
yadi na śṛṇoti badhiro ha bhavati vāco ha chidyate 6
yadi dūre syuḥ pratyudūhya gṛhṇīyāt 7
devīrāpa iti tṛṇamantardhāyābhijuhoti 8
yadi vā purā tṛṇaṃ syāttasmiñjuhuyāt 9
kārṣirasīti darbhairāhutimapaplāvya samudrasya vo 'kṣityā unnaya ityabhihutānāṃ maitrāvaruṇacamasena gṛhṇāti 10
somasya tvā mūjavato rasaṃ gṛhṇāmītyekadhanāḥ 11
patnī pannejanīrgṛhṇāti pratyaṅtiṣṭhantī vasubhyo rudrebhya ādityebhya iti 12
prehyudehīti neṣṭā patnīmudānayati | ehyudehīti vā 13
apareṇa neṣṭrīyaṃ patnī pannejanīḥ sādayati pratyaṅtiṣṭhantī vasubhyo rudrebhya ādityebhya iti 14
tā evamevācchāvākaṃ sīdantamanūpasādayati 15
iti pañcamī kaṇḍikā

hotṛcamasena vasatīvarībhyo niṣicyopari cātvāle hotṛcamasaṃ maitrāvaruṇacamasaṃ ca saṃsparśya vasatīvarīrvyānayati 1
samanyā yantītyabhijñāya hotṛcamasānmaitrāvaruṇacamasa ānayati | maitrāvaruṇacamasāddhotṛcamase | etadvā viparītam 2
upari cātvāle dhāryamāṇā ubhayīḥ pracaraṇyā samanakti saṃ vo'naktu varuṇaḥ samindraḥ saṃ pūṣā saṃ dhātā saṃ bṛhaspatiḥ | tvaṣṭā viṣṇuḥ prajayā saṃrarāṇo yajamānāya draviṇaṃ dadhātviti | yathāyathaṃ dhuro dhurbhiḥ kalpantāmiti 3
adhvaryo'verapā iti hotādhvaryuṃ pṛcchati 4
utemanannamuriti pratyuktvā pracaraṇīśeṣātkratukaraṇaṃ juhoti yamagne pṛtsu martyamiti 5
tadabhāve caturgṛhītena 6
yadyagniṣṭomo juhotītyuktam 7
athaikeṣām | yadyagniṣṭomo juhoti | yadyukthyaḥ paridhau nimārṣṭi | yadi ṣoḍaśī hutvā paridhau lepaṃ
nimṛjya droṇakalaśaṃ rarāṭīṃ copaspṛśati | na juhoti nopaspṛśati vājapeye 'tirātre ca | etadyajurvadansamudyaiva prapadyate 8
aparayā dvārā havirdhānamapaḥ prapādayati | pūrvayā gataśriyaḥ 9
pūrvayā yajamānaḥ prapadyate 10
dakṣiṇasya havirdhānasya pradhure pracaraṇīṃ sādayati 11
iti ṣaṣṭhī kaṇḍikā

yaṃ kāmayeta paṇḍakaḥ syāditi taṃ pracaraṇyopaspṛśet 1
etasyaiva havirdhānasyādhastātpuro'kṣaṃ maitrāvaruṇacamasam | uttarasyāṃ vartanyāṃ puraścakraṃ hotṛcamasam | uttarasya havirdhānasyādhastātpuro'kṣaṃ vasatīvarīḥ | paścādakṣameka
dhanāḥ 2
etadvā viparītam 3
apo yajamāno'nuprapadyate | yaśa evainamṛcchatīti vijñāyate 4
atra dadhigraheṇa carati 5
audumbareṇa gṛhṇāti 6
upayāmagṛhīto'si prajāpataye tvā jyotiṣmate jyotiṣmantaṃ gṛhṇāmīti dadhi gṛhītvāpendra dviṣato mana iti harati | prāṇāya tvāpānāya tveti juhoti 7
dadhigraho nityaḥ kāmyaśca | kāmyāvitarau 8
ājyagrahaṃ gṛhṇīyāttejaskāmasyetyuktam 9
yadi somagrahaṃ gṛhṇīyādekagrahāyāptaṃ rājānamupare nyupya vasatīvarībhirupasṛjyāvīvṛdhaṃ vo manasā sujātā ityabhimantrya tisro yahvasya samidhaḥ parijmano devā akṛṇvannuśijo amartyave | tāsāmekāmadadhurmartye bhujaṃ lokamiddve upa jāmī īyaturiti somakaraṇyābhiṣuṇoti 10
ā māskāniti prathamaplutamaṃśumabhimantrayate | drapsaścaskandeti vipruṣaḥ 11
hiraṇyapāṇirabhiṣuṇoti gṛhṇāti juhotītyatyantapradeśaḥ 12
tisro yahvasyetyetadvarjaṃ ca 13
taṃ hutvā sadasi pratyaṅmukho bhakṣayati bhakṣehīti yathāliṅgam 14
anadhikṛto vā somadharmairdadhigrahavikāratvāt 15
yadanyatsomakaraṇyāste somadharmāḥ 16
aṃśumadābhyaṃ vā prathamaṃ gṛhṇāti 17
śukraṃ te śukreṇa gṛhṇāmīti dadhnaḥ payaso nigrābhyāṇāṃ vā 18
upanaddhasya rājñastrīnaṃśūnpravṛhati 19
iti saptamī kaṇḍikā

vasavastvā pravṛhantu gāyatreṇa chandasetyetaiḥ pratimantram 1
tairenaṃ caturādhūnoti | pañcakṛtvaḥ saptakṛtvo vā | māndāsu ta ityetānprativibhajya 2
āsminnugrā acucyavurityādāya kakuhaṃ rūpamiti harati | yatte somādābhyaṃ nāma jāgṛvīti juhoti 3
ādhavanānaṃśūnprajñātānnidhāyośiktvaṃ deva soma gāyatreṇa chandasetyetaiḥ pratimantramanusavanamekaikaṃ mahābhiṣaveṣvapisṛjati 4
aṃśuṃ gṛhṇannekagrahāyāptaṃ rājānamupare nyupya sakṛdabhiṣutya vāmadevyaṃ manasā gāyamāno 'navānaṃ gṛhṇāti | vāmadevyasya varcā kayā naścitra ābhuvaditi 5
parācīnena grāhyaḥ prāṇatāpānatā vā prāṇyāpānya vyanatā vā 6
yadi vyavānedā naḥ prāṇa etu parāvata iti śatamānaṃ hiraṇyamabhivyaneyātāmadhvaryuryajamānaśca 7
athainau pratiprasthātā hiraṇyena saṃsparśayatyadbhiśca pratyukṣati 8
indrāgnī me varcaḥ kṛṇutāmityadhvaryurapa upaspṛśya dadhanve vā yadīmanvityaniruktayāprājāpatyayā prāṇyāpānya vyanañjuhoti 9
yadi na śaknuyādgrahītuṃ hotuṃ vā vare datte gṛhṇīyājjuhuyādvā 10
aṃśau dvādaśa prathamagarbhāḥ paṣṭhauhīrdadāti kṛttyadhī
vāsaṃ ca | evamadābhye 11
bhrātṛvyavatādābhyo grahītavyaḥ | bubhūṣatāṃśuḥ 12
tau na sarvatra grahītavyau | vājapeye rājasūye sattre sarvavedase vā 13
yo'sya supriyaḥ suvicita iva syāttasya grahītavyau 14
ityaṣṭamī kaṇḍikā iti dvitīyaḥ paṭalaḥ

uttarasyāṃ vartanyāṃ hotṛcamasaṃ vasatīvarībhirabhipūrya nigrābhyāsu yajamānaṃ vācayati nigrābhyā stha devaśruta iti 1
devasya tvā savituḥ prasava iti grāvāṇamupāṃśusavanamādāya grāvāsyadhvarakṛdityabhimantrayate | tamādadāno vācaṃ yatvāgrayaṇaṃ gṛhītvā visṛjate 2
athainamupare nidhāyāṃśubhirabhimimīte krayavat 3
etāvannānā | indrāya tvā vṛtraghna ityetaiḥ pratimantram 4
pañcakṛtvo yajuṣā mimīte | pañcakṛtvastūṣṇīm 5
nāṃśūnupasamūhati 6
bhūyāṃsaṃ prātaḥsavanāya rājānaṃ prakalpayati | alpīyāṃsaṃ mādhyaṃdināya 7
upanahya pratyāropyaikagrahāyāptaṃ rājānamupare nyupya hotṛcamase'ṃśūnavadhāya tasmingrāvāṇamupāṃśusavanamupari dhārayaṃstriḥ pradakṣiṇaṃ pariplāvayannigrābhamupaiti prāgapāgudagadharāgiti 8
yāṃ bhāryāṃ kāmayeta tāṃ manasā dhyāyedamba niṣvareti |
sā hainaṃ kāmayate 9
śvātrā stha vṛtratura iti tāsāmekadeśenopasṛjyopaspṛṣṭasya rājñaḥ ṣaḍaṃśūnārdrānsaṃśliṣṭānādāya carmaṇi nidhāya yatte soma divi jyotiriti rājānamabhimantrayate 10
iti navamī kaṇḍikā

dhiṣaṇe vīḍū ityadhiṣavaṇaphalake 1
avīvṛdhaṃ vo manasā sujātā iti rājānamevābhimantrya mā bhermā saṃvikthā iti grāvāṇamudyamyānāgasastvā vayamindreṇa preṣitā upa vāyuṣṭe astvaṃśabhūrmitraste astvaṃśabhūrvaruṇaste astvaṃśabhūrahataḥ somo rājeti tṛṇamantardhāyābhiṣuṇoti 2
yadi vā purā tṛṇaṃ kāṣṭhaṃ mūlaṃ vā syāttasminpraharet 3
aṣṭaukṛtvo'gre'bhiṣuṇoti 4
atha pratiprasthātopāṃśupātraṃ dhārayannapāttānāmupari dvāvaṃśū antardadhāti 5
tasminnabhiṣutamadhvaryurañjalinā gṛhṇāti vācaspataye pavasva vājinniti 6
pavitramupayāmaḥ sādanaṃ ca na vidyate 7
eṣa prathamaḥ paryāyaḥ | evaṃ vihito dvitīyastṛtī yaśca 8
api vaikādaśakṛtvo dvitīyamabhiṣuṇoti | dvādaśakṛtvastṛtīyam 9
dvirādito'ntato vā nigrābhopāyanamupasargaśca 10
hotṛcamasīyānaṃśūnuttame paryāye'bhiṣuṇoti 11
avaśiṣṭānāṃ pratiprasthātā dvaudvāvaṃśū antardadhāti 12
svāṃkṛto'sītyadhvaryurgrahamādāyorvantarikṣamanvihīti dakṣiṇena hotāramatikrāmati 13
yena vā hotā pratipādayet 14
manastvāṣṭviti dakṣiṇato'vasthāya dakṣiṇaṃ paridhisaṃdhimanvavahṛtya 15
iti daśamī kaṇḍikā

svāhā tvā subhavaḥ sūryāyeti dakṣiṇataḥ prāñcamṛjuṃ saṃtataṃ dīrghaṃ hutvā devebhyastvā marīcipebhya iti madhyame paridhau lepaṃ nimārṣṭi 1
yaṃ dviṣyāttasya prahvo juhuyāt 2
yadi kāmayeta varṣukaḥ parjanyaḥ syādityabhyantaraṃ pātrasyāvamṛjyābhyantaraṃ paridhernīcā hastena nimṛjyāt 3
yadi kāmayetāvarṣukaḥ syāditi bāhyataḥ pātrasyordhvamunmṛjya bāhyataḥ paridheruttānena hastenordhvamunmṛjyāt 4
sarvamāgrayaṇasthālyāṃ saṃpātamavanīyaiṣa te yoniḥ prāṇāya tveti riktaṃ pātramāyatane sādayitvā tasminnaṃśumavāsya taṃ tṛtīyasavane'pisṛjyābhiṣuṇuyāt 5
athaitānyabhicarataḥ 6
upāṃśuṃ gṛhītvāmuṣya tvā prāṇe sādayāmīti sādayitvā devasya tvā savituḥ prasava ityādāyāmuṣya tvā prāṇamapidadhāmīti hastenāpidhāyāmuṃ jahyatha tvā hoṣyāmīti brūyāt 7
yadi dūre syādā tamitostiṣṭhet 8
praharṣiṇo madirasya made mṛṣāsāvastviti jihmastiṣṭhanhutvāmuṣya tvā prāṇe sādayāmīti sādayet 9
yo vastre bāhāvurasi vāṃśurāśliṣṭastamabhicarato juhotītyeke devāṃśo yasmai tveḍe tatsatyamapariplutā bhaṅgyena hato'sau phaḍiti 10
yatte somādābhyaṃ nāma jāgṛvītyupāṃśupāvanānāmanusavanaṃ dvaudvāvaṃśū mahābhiṣaveṣvapisṛjati 11
ityekādaśī kaṇḍikā iti tṛtīyaḥ paṭalaḥ

adābhyāṃśumupāṃśupāvanau cāpisṛjya sarve'dhvaryavo digbhyo mahābhiṣavamabhiṣuṇvanti 1
purastādadhvaryurdakṣiṇataḥ pratiprasthātā paścānneṣṭottarata unnetā | paścādadhvaryuḥ purastānneṣṭetyeke 2
upare rājānaṃ nyupya hotṛcamase'ṃśūnavadhāyetyetadādyopasargādupāṃśusavanavarjam | tūṣṇīmitarairgrāvabhirabhiṣuṇvanti 3
evaṃ dvitīyaṃ tṛtīyaṃ copasṛjyābhiṣuṇvanti 4
abhiṣutamadhvaryurañjalinā saṃsiñcati 5
tamunnetāntareṣeṇoddhṛtyottarata ādhavanīye'vanayati 6
eṣa evāpāṃ somasya ca panthāḥ 7
eṣa prathamaḥ paryāyaḥ 8
triparyāyaḥ 10
saṃbhṛtya rājānamupare grāvṇaḥ saṃmukhānkṛtvā prapīḍyarjīṣaṃ mukheṣūpohati | ghāsamebhyaḥ prayacchatīti vijñāyate 11
teṣūdgātāro droṇakalaśaṃ pratiṣṭhāpya tasminnudīcīnadaśaṃ pavitraṃ vitanvanti 12
pavitraṃ te vitataṃ brahmaṇaspate prabhurgātrāṇi paryeṣi
viśvataḥ | ataptatanūrna tadāmo aśnute śṛtāsa idvahantastatsamāśateti vitatyamānamabhimantrayate yajamānaḥ 13
iti dvādaśī kaṇḍikā

pavitrasya yajamāno nābhiṃ kṛtvā tasminhotṛcamasena dhārāṃ srāvayati 1
udacanenonnetādhavanīyāddhotṛcamasa ānayati 2
saṃtatā dhārā srāvayitavyā | kāmo hāsya samardhuko bhavatīti vijñāyate 3
yaṃ dviṣyāttasya vicchindyāt 4
dhārāyā antaryāmaṃ gṛhṇāti | sarvāṃścāto grahānā dhruvāt 5
samānabrāhmaṇāvupāṃśvantaryāmau sābhicaraṇikau 6
etāvannānā | upayāmagṛhīto'syantaryaccheti gṛhītvottareṇa hotāramatikrāmati | yena vā hotā pratipādayeduttarato'vasthāyottaraṃ paridhisaṃdhimanvavahṛtyottarārdhe juhoti 7
viparītau deśāveke samāmananti 8
asarvamāgrayaṇasthālyāṃ saṃpātamavanīyaiṣa te yonirapānāya tvetyariktaṃ pātramāyatane sādayitvā vyānāya tveti te antareṇa grāvāṇamupāṃśusavanaṃ dakṣiṇāmukhaṃ saṃspṛṣṭaṃ pātrābhyām 9
yaṃ kāmayeta pramāyukaḥ syādityasaṃspṛṣṭau tasyetyuktam 10
nānudite sūrya upāṃśvantaryāmau juhuyāt 11
yadi tvaretānudita upāṃśuṃ juhuyādudite'ntaryāmam 12
ubhāvanudite hotavyāvityeke 13
iti trayodaśī kaṇḍikā iti caturthaḥ paṭalaḥ

yadi rathaṃtarasāmā somaḥ syādaindravāyavāgrāngṛhṇīyāt | yadi bṛhatsāmā śukrāgrān | yadi jagatsāmāgrayaṇāgrān 1
yadyubhayasāmā yāthākāmī 2
api vaindravāyavāgrāneva 3
aindravāyavāgrāngṛhṇīyādyaḥ krāmayeta yathāpūrvaṃ prajāḥ kalperanniti | kāmyāni grahāgrāṇi 4
yānprācīnamāgrayaṇādityuktam 5
yaṃ kāmyamaindravāyavātpūrvaṃ gṛhṇīyānna sādayet 6
aindravāyavasya sādanamanu sādyate 7
aindravāyavaṃ gṛhṇāti 8
ā vāyo bhūṣa śucipā ityanudrutyopayāmagṛhīto'si vāyave tveti gṛhītvāpayamyendravāyū ime sutā ityanudrutyopayāmagṛhīto'sīndravāyubhyāṃ tveti gṛhītvā pavitradaśābhiḥ parimṛjyaiṣa te yoniḥ sajoṣābhyāṃ tveti sādayati 9
sarvāngrahānpavitradaśābhiḥ parimṛjyaiṣa te yoniriti yathādevataṃ yathāyatanaṃ sādayati 10
etadarthaṃ vā dvitīyaṃ pavitraṃ daśāvatsyāt 11
ayaṃ vāṃ mitrāvaruṇeti maitrāvaruṇaṃ gṛhītvā rāyā vayaṃ sasavāṃso madema havyena devā yavasena gāvaḥ | tāṃ dhenuṃ mitrāvaruṇā yuvaṃ no viśvāhā dhattamanapasphurantīmiti śṛtaśītena payasā śrītvaiṣa te yonirṛtāyubhyāṃ
tveti sādayati 12
ayaṃ venaścodayaditi śukraṃ gṛhītvā hiraṇyena śrītvaiṣa te yonirvīratāṃ pāhīti sādayati 13
śaṇḍāya tveti dveṣyasya 14
taṃ pratnatheti manthinaṃ gṛhītvā mano na yeṣu havaneṣu juhvadvipaḥ śacyā vanutho dravantā | ā yaḥ śaryābhistuvinṛmṇo asyāśrīṇītādiśaṃ gabhastāviti saktubhiḥ śrīṇātyanabhidhvaṃsayannātmānam | itarāṃśca grahān 15
eṣa te yoniḥ prajāḥ pāhīti sādayati 16
iti caturdaśī kaṇḍikā

markāya tveti dveṣyasya 1
yadi kāmayeta yo grāme taṃ grāmānnirūheyaṃ yo bahirgrāmāttaṃ grāme kuryāmitīdamahamamumāmuṣyāyaṇamamuṣya putramamuṣyā viśa udūhāmīti śukramudūhyedamahamamumāmuṣyāyaṇamamuṣya putramamuṣyāṃ viśi sādayāmīti tasminmanthinaṃ sādayet 2
ye devā divītyupariṣṭādupayāmayā purastādupayāmena vā yajuṣā dvābhyāṃ dhārābhyāṃ sthālyāgrayaṇaṃ gṛhṇāti 3
ya āgrayaṇasthālyāṃ somastamanyasminpātra ānīya tāṃ dvitīyāṃ dhārāṃ karoti 4
triṃśattrayaśceti rugṇavatyarcā bhrātṛvyavato'bhicarato vā gṛhṇīyāt 5
vidadyatī saramā rugṇamadrermahi pāthaḥ pūrvyaṃ sadhriyakkaḥ | agraṃ nayatsupadyakṣarāṇāmacchā ravaṃ prathamā jānatī gāditi vābhicarataḥ 6
āgrayaṇaṃ gṛhītvā trirhiṅkṛtya vācaṃ visṛjate 7
somaḥ pavate somaḥ pavate somaḥ pavate subhūtāya pavate brahmavarcasāya pavate'smai brahmaṇe pavate'smai kṣatrāya pavate'syai viśe pavate'dbhyaḥ pavata oṣadhībhyaḥ pavate vanaspatibhyaḥ pavate dyāvāpṛthivībhyāṃ pavate'smai sunvate yajamānāya pavate mahyaṃ jyaiṣṭhyāya pavate | yathā devebhyo'pavathā evaṃ mahyaṃ pavasveti trirudvadati śanairuccairatha sūccaiḥ 8
eṣa te yonirviśvebhyastvā devebhya iti sādayitvā trīnagniṣṭome'tigrāhyāngṛhṇāti | āgneyamaindraṃ saurya miti 9
agna āyūṃṣyuttiṣṭhaṃstaraṇiriti grahaṇasādanāḥ 10
sthālyokthyaṃ gṛhṇāti | upayāmagṛhīto'sīndrāya tvā bṛhadvate vayasvata iti grahaṇasādanau 11
iti pañcadaśī kaṇḍikā

mūrdhānaṃ divo aratiṃ pṛthivyā iti sthālyā dhruvaṃ pūrṇaṃ gṛhṇāti 1
alpaṃ gṛhṇīyādyaṃ kāmayeta pramāyukaḥ syāditi | uparyardhaṃ yaṃ kāmayetottaramāyuriyāditi 2
eṣa te yoniragnaye tvā vaiśvānarāyetyāyatane hiraṇye sādayedāyuṣkāmasya 3
taṃ rājaputro gopāyatyāvanayanāt 4
yadi kāmayetāvagatamaparundhyuraparuddho'vagaccheditīdamahamamumāmuṣyāyaṇamamuṣya putramamuṣyā viśa utkhidāmīti dhruvamutkhidyedamahamamumāmuṣyāyaṇamamuṣya putramamuṣyāṃ viśi sādayāmīti tatraiva punaḥ sādayet 5
yadevaṃ kuryādāyuḥ prajānāṃ vicālayet | tṛṇametena mantreṇoparyuparyatiharet 6
yadhabhicareidamahamamuṣyāmuṣyāyaṇasyāyuḥ pravartayāmīti dhruvaṃ pravartayet 7
dhruvaṃ tvā dhruvakṣitimamumā sthānāccyāvayāmīti vā vyaṅgayet 8
atra dhārā viramati 9
prapīḍya pavitraṃ nidadhāti 10
ekadhanānāṃ yathārthaṃ sarvāśca maitrāvaruṇacamasīyā ādhavanīye'vanīya pūtabhṛto bila udīcīnadaśaṃ pavitraṃ vitatya ya ādhavanīye rājā tamasarvaṃ pūtabhṛtyavanīyopayāmagṛhīto'si prajāpataye tveti droṇakalaśamabhimṛśet | indrāya tvetyādhavanīyam | viśvebhyastvā devebhya iti pūtabhṛtam 11
te pavamānagrahāḥ 12
purastādupayāmāḥ sarve 13
pañcahotrā yajamānaḥ sarvāngrahānabhimṛśati 14
drapsaścaskanda yaste drapso yo drapso yaste drapsa ityetaiḥ pratimantraṃ vaipruṣānhomāñjuhoti 15
prathamaṃ sarvatrānuṣaktamuttarāṃstrīnvahṛtānanusavanameke samāmananti 16
saptahotāraṃ manasānudrutyāhavanīye sagrahaṃ hutvodañcaḥ
prahvā bahiṣpavamānāya pañcartvijaḥ samanvārabdhāḥ sarpanti 17
iti ṣoḍaśī kaṇḍikā

adhvaryuṃ prastotānvārabhate prastotāraṃ pratihartā pratihartāramudgātodgātāraṃ brahmā brahmāṇaṃ yajamānaḥ 1
yadyu vai svayaṃ hotā yajamānaḥ syātsarpedeva | aupagātraṃ hyasyeti bṛhvṛcabrāhmaṇaṃ bhavati 2
tsaranta iva rehāṇā iva nyaṅṅiva śīrṣāṇi kṛtvā sarpanti 3
mṛga iva hi yajñaḥ | pūrvo'dhvaryurbarhirmuṣṭiṃ dhūnvansarpati vāgagregā agra etviti gāyatraḥ panthā vasavo devatāvṛkeṇāparipareṇa pathā svasti vasūnaśīyet 4
cātvālamavekṣamāṇāḥ stuvate | uttare vā vedyaṃse 5
athādhvaryuḥ stotramupākaroti 6
sarveṣu pavamāneṣvevamupākaraṇaḥ 8
asarjyasarji vāgasarjyaindraṃ saho'sarjyupāvartadhvamiti barhirbhyāmanyāni pavamānebhyaḥ stotrāṇyupākaroti 9
tasmādbrāhmaṇena bahiṣpavamāna upasadyaḥ | pavitraṃ hi | yaṃ dviṣyāttaṃ bahiṣpavamānātparibādheteti vijñāyate 10
stūyamānaṃ yajamāna upagāyati | catvāro'varārdhyā upagātāraḥ 11
nādhvaryuḥ 12
vasvyai hiṅkuru tasyai prastuhi tasyai stuhi tasyai me'varuddhyā iti purastādbahiṣpavamānādyajamāno japati 13
daśahotāraṃ ca vyācaṣṭe | stūyamāne ca daśahotāraṃ japati 14
śyeno'si gāyatracchandā iti madhyamāyāṃ ca stotrī yāyāmanvāroham 15
dvitīye pavamāne dvitīyena mantreṇa | tṛtīye tṛtīyena 16
athātyantapradeśaḥ | stutasya stutamasīti stotramanumantrayate | śastrasya śastramasīti śastram 17
indriyāvanto vanāmaha ityubhayatrānuṣajati 18
stute'dhvaryuḥ saṃpreṣyatyagnīdagnīnvihara barhi stṛṇāhi puroḍāśām̐ alaṃkurviti 19
athaikeṣām | stuta uttiṣṭhannāhāgnīdagnīnvihara barhi stṛṇīhi puroḍāśām̐ alaṃkuru pratiprasthātaḥ paśunehīti 20
saṃpraiṣavatkurvanti 21
iti saptadaśī kaṇḍikā iti pañcamaḥ paṭalaḥ

āgnīdhrāddhiṣṇiyānviharati 1
aṅgārairdve savane | śalākābhistṛtīyam 2
pāṃsudhiṣṇiyeṣu nivapati 3
tenānupūrvyeṇa yathānyuptā bhavanti 4
pracaraṇyāṃ pañcagṛhītaṃ gṛhītvā droṇakalaśācca pariplavayā rājānaṃ purastātpratyaṅṅāsīno dhiṣṇiyānvyāghārayati taireva mantraiḥ | tūṣṇīṃ vā 5
āhavanīyamāgnīdhrīyaṃ hotrīyaṃ mārjālīyamiti somena | ājyenetarān 6
yajñasya saṃtatirasi yajñasya tvā saṃtatyai stṛṇāmi saṃtatyai tvā yajñasyeti gārhapatyātprakramya saṃtatamanupṛṣṭhyaṃ barhiḥ stṛṇātyāhavanīyāt 7

prāṇāya ma ityupāṃśum | apānāya ma ityantaryāmam | vyānāya ma ityupāṃśusavanam | vāce ma ityaindravāyavam |
dakṣakratubhyāṃ ma iti maitrāvaruṇam | cakṣurbhyāṃ ma iti śukrāmanthinau | śrotrāya ma ityāśinam | ātmane ma ityāgrayaṇam | aṅgebhyo ma ityukthyam | āyuṣe ma iti dhruvam | tejase me varcodā varcase pavasvettyājyāni | paśubhyo me varcodā varcase pavasveti pṛṣadājyam | puṣṭyai me varcodāḥ pavadhvamiti sarvāngrahān | stanābhyāṃ me varcodau varcase me pavethāmityṛtupātre |

ko'si ko nāmetyāhavanīyam 1
soma tvāṃ vṛṇīmaha udgātāraṃ nṛcakṣasaṃ pārayā ṇa svastaye | viśvebhyo me rūpebhya iti sarvaṃ rājānam 2
bubhūṣannavekṣeta | brahmavarcasakāma āmayāvyabhicaranvā 3
śṛtaṃkārairyajamānaḥ sarvāngrahānupatiṣṭhate 4
śṛtau sthaḥ prāṇāpānau me śrīṇītamityupāṃśvantaryāmau | śṛto'si vyānaṃ me śrīṇāhītyupāṃśusavanam | śṛto'si vācaṃ me śrīṇāhītyaindravāyavam | śṛto'si dakṣakratū me śrīṇāhīti maitrāvaruṇam | śṛtau sthaścakṣuṣī me śrīṇītamiti śukrāmanthinau | śṛto'si śrotraṃ me śrīṇāhītyāśvinam | śṛto'syātmānaṃ me śrīṇāhītyāgrayaṇam | śṛto'syaṅgāni me śrīṇāhītyukthyam | śṛto'syāyurme śrīṇāhīti dhruvam | śṛtamasi tejo ma śrīṇāhītyājyāni | śṛtamasi paśūnme śrīṇāhīti pṛṣadājyam | śṛtā stha puṣṭiṃ me śrīṇīteti sarvāngrahān | prajāpaterjaṭharamasi śṛto'si sa mā śrīṇāhīti droṇakalaśam | indrasya jaṭharamasi śṛto'si sa mā śrīṇāhītyādhavanīyam | viśveṣāṃ devānāṃ jaṭharamasi śṛto'si sa mā śrīṇāhīti pūtabhṛtam | śṛtastvaṃ śṛto'haṃ śṛto me prāṇaḥ śṛto me'pānaḥ śṛto me vyānaḥ śṛtaṃ me cakṣuḥ śṛtaṃ me śrotaṃ śṛtā me vāk śṛto ma ātmā śṛtaṃ me haviḥ śṛto me somaḥ śṛtā me grahāḥ | imamindra sutaṃ piba jyeṣṭhamamartyaṃ madam | śukrasya tvābhyakṣarandhārā ṛtasya sādane | vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi vṛṣā deva vṛṣavrataḥ | vṛṣā dharmāṇi dadhiṣe | vṛṣṇaste vṛṣṇyaṃ śavo vṛṣā vane vṛṣā made | sa tvaṃ vṛṣanvṛṣedasi | aśvo na cakrado vṛṣā saṃ gā indro samarvataḥ | vi no rāye duro vṛdhīti sarvaṃ rājānam 5
agniḥ pavitraṃ sa mā punātu | somaḥ pavitraṃ sa mā punātu | sūryaḥ pavitraṃ sa mā punātu | upahūtā gāva upahūto'haṃ gavāmityetairyathāliṅgamupasthāya sphyaḥ svastirityutkare vedikaraṇāni parāsyopatiṣṭhate 6

kalaśa iti kalaśam | soma iti somam | agnirityagnim | upa devā iti devān | upa yajña iti yajñam | upa mā hotrā iti hotrakān 1

tataḥ śukrāmanthibhyāṃ pracarataḥ | stuto'si janadhā devāstvā śukrapāḥ praṇayantviti śukramadhvaryurādatte | stuto'si janadhā devāstvā manthipāḥ praṇayantviti manthinaṃ pratiprasthātā | camasāṃścamasādhvaryavaḥ 1
tau prokṣitābhyāṃ śakalābhyāmapidhāyāprokṣitābhyāmadhastātpāṃsūnapadhvaṃsayato'panuttau śaṇḍāmarkau sahāmuneti | apanuttaḥ śaṇḍa iti vādhvaryurdveṣyaṃ manasā dhyāyan |

atra savanīyānāṃ śākhāpraharaṇaṃ vājinacaryāntarvedi yajamānasya vedābhimarśanaṃ piṣṭalepaphalīkaraṇahomau patnyā vedaprāsanaṃ praṇītāsu mārjanamupaveṣodasanaṃ kapālavimocanamiti kriyante 13
nahyeteṣāṃ prāsaṅgikaṃ vaiśeṣikamutkarṣati 14
utkarṣedityaparam 15
vaṣaṭkāreṇa hotā bhakṣaṃ labhate 16
homābhiṣavābhyāmadhvaryuḥ | nānyatareṇa 17
samākhyānenāpi bhakṣaṃ labhante 18
sarvāṃścamasānsakṛddhotā bhakṣayati 19
dviḥ svaṃ camasam 20
hotrakāḥ svaṃsvaṃ camasaṃ dvirbhakṣayanti 21
tānadhvaryuḥ sakṛdbhakṣitānyathāpūrvaṃ pratibhakṣayati 22
antata itare 23
bhakṣitānāpyāyayantyāpyāyasva sametu ta iti 24
te nārāśaṃsāḥ 25
dvinārāśaṃse pūrve savane bhavataḥ | ekanārāśaṃsaṃ tṛtīyasavanam 26
bhakṣitāpyāyitānsādayanti dakṣiṇasya havirdhānasyādhastādavālambe 27
iti pañcaviṃśī kaṇḍikā iti saptamaḥ paṭalaḥ

upaviśatyacchāvāko bahiḥ sadaso'greṇa svaṃ dhiṣṇiyam 1
tasmai puroḍāśaśakalamādadhadāhācchāvāka vadasva yatte vādyamiti 2
yadāsya vijānātyupo asmānbrāhmaṇānbrāhmaṇā hvayadhvamityathainaṃ hotra āvedayatyacchāvāko vā ayamupahavamicchate taṃ hotarupahvayasveti 3
uta no gāva upahūtā upahūteti hoturabhijñāyonnīyamānāyānubrūhyacchāvākasya camasādhvaryo unnayasvobhayataḥ śukraṃ kuruṣveti 4
tamādāyāhavanīyaṃ gatvāśrāvya pratyāśrāvite saṃpreṣyatyacchāvāka yajet 5
vaṣaṭkṛtānuvaṣaṭkṛte hutvā harati bhakṣam 6
tena na saṃbhakṣayati | nāsminnupahavamicchate | yadyasminnupahavamiccheta bhakṣayetyenaṃ brūyāt 7
bhakṣitāpyāyitamantarā neṣṭurāgnīdhrasya ca camasau sādayitvartugrahaiḥ pracarataḥ 8
droṇakalaśādgṛhyante | na sādyante 9
pūrveṣāṃ śeṣeṣūttarānabhigṛhṇītaḥ 10
pūrvo'dhvaryurgṛhṇāti jaghanyaḥ pratiprasthātopayāmagṛhīto'si madhuścetyetaiḥ pratimantram 11
madhave tvā mādhavāya tvetyeke samāmananti 12
purastādupayāmāḥ sarve 13
nānyo'nyamanuprapadyeta | prasiddhamevādhvaryurdakṣiṇena prapadyate | prasiddhaṃ pratiprasthātottareṇa 14
dvāryeva vyatītaḥ 15
praviśantamevādhvaryuṃ pratiprasthātā graheṇa paripragṛhṇāti | niṣkrāmantaṃ pātreṇa 16
ṛtunā preṣyeti triṣvādyeṣvadhvaryuḥ saṃpreṣyati | evaṃ pratiprasthātā 17
pātrayormukhe paryāvṛtyartubhiḥ preṣyeti dvayoradhvaryuḥ | evaṃ pratiprasthātā 18
punaḥ paryāvṛtyartunā preṣyeti sakṛdadhvaryuḥ | evaṃ pratiprasthātā 19
iti ṣaḍviṃśī kaṇḍikā

dvādaśa trayodaśa caturdaśa vā gṛhyante 1
dvādaśasu saha prathamau gṛhyete | sahottamau 2
trayodaśasūttamayoḥ saha grahaṇapradāne 3
tathā caturdaśasu prathamottamayoḥ 4
saṃsarpo'syaṃhaspatyāya tveti trayodaśacaturdaśau vāvagṛhyete 5
adhvaryū yajataṃ gṛhapate yajetyabhijñāyobhayatrātipreṣyati hotaretadyajeti 6
evaṃ gṛhapatiḥ sve praiṣānte 7
dvidevatyavatsaṃpātau vyavanīyābhakṣitena pātreṇādhva
ryuraindrāgnaṃ gṛhṇāti | indrāgnī āgataṃ sutamiti grahaṇasādanau | pratiprasthātā harati bhakṣam 8
ubhāvadhvaryū yathāvaṣaṭkṛtaṃ pratibhakṣayataḥ 9
sarveṣāṃ somapātrāṇāṃ bhakṣitānāṃ mārjālīye prakṣālanam 10
etatpātramādāyādhvaryuḥ sadobile prāṅmukha upaviśyeḍā devahūriti śastraṃ pratigariṣyañjapati 11
te śaoṃsāvomiti hoturabhijñāya pradakṣiṇamāvartamānaḥ śaoṃsā moda iveti pratyāhvayate| śaṃsā moda iveti vā 12
ṛtupātraṃ dhārayamāṇaḥ sadobile pratyaṅtiṣṭhanprati
gṛṇāti | prahvo vā 13
othā moda ivetyardharceṣu | omothā moda ivetyavasāneṣu | praṇava evāntaḥ 14
othā moda ivaṃ hotarmoda ivamothā moda ivomiti vikalpante 15
nārdharcāllupyate | nābhipratigṛṇāti 16
śaoṃsā moda ivauthā moda iveti vyāhāveṣūbhayaṃ karoti 17
śastraṃ pratigīrya imadhvayurādatte | camasāṃścamasādhvaryavaḥ 18
āśrāvya pratyāśrāvite saṃpreṣyatyukthaśā yaja somasyeti | vaṣaṭkṛte juhoti 19
iti saptaviṃśī kaṇḍikā

anuprakampayanti nārāśaṃsānvaṣaṭkārānuvaṣaṭkārau 1
bhakṣānharanti 2
vyākhyāto grahasya bhakṣaḥ | tathā nārāśaṃsānāṃ bhakṣaṇāpyāyanasādanāni 3
vaiśvadevaṃ śukrapātreṇa gṛhṇāti | omāsaścarṣaṇīdhṛta iti grahaṇasādanau 4
asarjyasarjīti barhirbhyāṃ stotramupākaroti 5
iḍāyai hiṅkuru tasyai prastuhi tasyai stuhi tasyai me'varuddhyā iti purastādājyānāṃ yajamāno japati caturhotāraṃ ca vyācaṣṭe 6
stūyamāne ca caturhotāraṃ japati 7
caturhotṝnvyākhyāyājyairudgāyatīti vijñāyate 8
stuta ṛtupātravarjamaindrāgnavacchastrapratigaro grahanārāśaṃsāśca 9
sarvabhakṣāścamasā bhavanti 10
upayāmagṛhīto'si mitrāvaruṇābhyāṃ tvā juṣṭaṃ gṛhṇāmi devebhyo devā yuvamukthyebhya ukthyā uvamityu
kthyapātreṇokthyatṛtīyaṃ gṛhītvaiṣa te yonirmitrāvaruṇābhyāṃ tveti sādayitvā punarhavirasīti sthālīmabhimṛśati 11
yanmukhyāścamasā bhavanti tasya pratigṛṇāti taṃ ca pratibhakṣayati 12
maitrāvaruṇacamasamukhyāṃścamasānunnīya pūrvavatstotramupākaroti 13
stute pūrvavacchastraṃ pratigīrya grahamadhvaryurādatte | camasāṃścamasādhvaryavaḥ | āśrāvya pratyāśrāvite saṃpreṣyatyukthaśā yaja somānāmiti | vaṣaṭkṛtānuvaṣaṭkṛte juhvati | bhakṣānharanti 14
evamata ūrdhvaṃ nārāśaṃsavarjaṃ gaṇeṣu caryā 15
devebhyastvā devā yuvaṃ pṛṇajmi yajñasyāyuṣa iti mukhye saṃpātamavanayati 16
ityaṣṭāviṃśī kaṇḍikā

yadi kāmayetādhvaryurātmānaṃ yajñayaśasenārpayeyamityuktam 1
evaṃ vihitāvuttarau paryāyau 2
tābhyāṃ pratiprasthātā carati | etāvannānā 3
indrāya tveti dvitīye grahaṇasādanau saṃnamatyardhaṃ cokthyaśeṣasya gṛhṇāti | pūrvavatsthālīmabhimṛśati | brāhmaṇācchaṃsicamasamukhyāṃścamasānunnayati 4
nābhakṣitaṃ camasaṃ stotreṇābhyupākaroti 5
na pratiprasthātordhvapātrasya bhakṣayati 6
mukhye saṃpātamavanayati 7
indrāgnibhyāṃ tveti tṛtīye grahaṇasādanau saṃnamati sarvaṃ cokthyaśeṣam gṛhṇāti | na sthālīmabhimṛśati 8
pūtabhṛto bila udīcīnadaśaṃ pavitraṃ vitatya ya ādhavanīye rājā taṃ sarvaṃ pūtabhṛtyavanīyācchāvākacamasamukhyāṃścamasānunnayansarvaṃ rājānamunnīya daśābhiḥ kalaśau mṛṣṭvā nyubjati 9
mukhye saṃpātamavanayati 10
ukthaśā ityāha prātaḥsavanaṃ pratigīrya śastraṃśastraṃ vā 11
asaṃtvaramāṇāḥ pūrvābhyāṃ savanābhyāṃ caranti | saṃtvaramāṇāstṛtīyasavanena 12
agniḥ prātaḥsavane pātvasmāniti saṃsthite savana āhutiṃ juhoti 13
praśāstaḥ prasuva prasuhīti vā saṃpreṣyati 14
sarpateti pratyāha 15
yena prasarpanti tena niḥsarpanti 16
saṃtiṣṭhate prātaḥsavanaṃ prātaḥsavanam 17
ityekonatriṃśī kaṇḍikā ityaṣṭamaḥ paṭalaḥ
iti dvādaśaḥ praśnaḥ

abhiṣavādi mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanaṃ tāyate 1
tasya prātaḥsavanena kalpo vyākhyātaḥ 2
hotṛcamasena vasatīvarībhyo niḥṣicya nigrābhyāḥ karoti 3
dvidevatyartugrahā darvihomāśca na vidyante 4
visrasya rājānaṃ grāvastute somoṣṇīṣaṃ prayacchati 5
asaṃpreṣito grāvastotrīyā anvāha 6
tathaiva mahābhiṣavaḥ 7
ghoṣavāṃstu 8
saṃrādhayantaścābhiṣuṇvantīhā3
iheti 9
uttamasyābhiṣavasya madhyame paryāye bṛhaddadhāti bṛhadbṛhaditi 10
uttame'bhiṣave 'bhiṣute rājanyasaṃbhṛte devā grāvāṇa indurindra ityavādiṣuḥ | endramacucyavuḥ paramasyāḥ parābataḥ | āsmātsadhastādororantarikṣāt | ā subhūtamasuṣavurbrahmavarcasaṃ ma āsuṣavuḥ samare rakṣāṃsyavadhiṣurapahataṃ brahmajyasyeti pratiprasthātā grāvṇo'numodate 11
paśupuroḍāśaṃ nirupya payasyāvarjaṃ savanīyāḥ 12
tena pracarya savanīyaiḥ pracarati 13
samānaṃ tu sviṣṭakṛdiḍam 14
sa kṛtākṛtaḥ 15
saṃbharaṇādyā dhārāyāḥ kṛte'dhvaryurgrahāngṛhṇāti 16
iti prathamā kaṇḍikā

śukrāmanthināvathāgrayaṇaṃ tisṛbhyo dhārābhyaḥ 1
āgrayaṇādutsicya dvitīyāṃ dhārāṃ karoti | udacanāttṛtīyām 2
ukthyaṃ gṛhītvā marutvatīyau | etadvā viparītam | madhya ukthyamabhito marutvatīyāvityeke 3
marutvantamiti svenartupātreṇādhvaryuḥ pūrvaṃ marutvatīyaṃ gṛhṇāti | indra marutva iti svena pratiprasthātottaram 4
tayoranyadevatāni grahaṇāni dveṣyasyaike samāmananti 5
viramati dhāraikadhanānāṃ yathārthamityetadādyā pañcahotuḥ 6
grahāvakāśaiḥ śṛtaṃkāraiścopasthāya vaipruṣānsaptahotāraṃ ca hutvā bahiṣpavamānavanmādhyaṃdine pavamānaṃ sarpanti 7
traiṣṭubhaḥ panthā rudrā devatāvṛkeṇāparipareṇa pathā svasti rudrānaśīyeti sarpaṇe vikāraḥ 8
uttareṇa havirdhānaṃ gatvā dakṣiṇena mārjālīyaṃ dhiṣṇiyaṃ parītya pūrvayā dvārā sadaḥ praviśyāgreṇa hotāramadhvaryuryajamānaścāvatiṣṭhete | dakṣiṇenottareṇa vā praśāsturdhiṣṇiyaṃ parītyodgātāro mādhyaṃdinena pavamānena stuvate 9
iti dvitīyā kaṇḍikā

jyotiṣe hiṅkuru tasyai prastuhi tasyai stuhi tasyai me'varuddhyā iti purastānmādhyaṃdinātpavamānādyajamāno japati | caturhotāraṃ pañcahotāraṃ vā vyācaṣṭe | jyoktyai hiṅkuru tasyai prastuhi tasyai stuhi tasyai me'varuddhyā iti ca | stūyamāne ca caturhotāraṃ pañcahotāraṃ vā japati | madhyamāyāṃ ca stotrīyāyāṃ dvitīyamanvāroham | stute'dhvaryuḥ saṃpreṣyatyagnīdagnīnvihara barhi stṛṇīhi puroḍāśām̐ alaṃkuru pratiprasthātardadhigharmeṇānūdehīti 1
āgnīdhre pratiprasthātā dadhigharmaṃ gṛhṇāti 2
audumbaryāṃ srucyupastīrya yāvatī dyāvāpṛthivī iti dadhi gṛhītvābhighārya vākca tvā manaśca śrīṇītāṃ prāṇaśca tvāpānaśca śrīṇītāṃ cakṣuśca tvā śrotraṃ ca śrīṇītāṃ dakṣaśca tvā balaṃ ca śrīṇītāmojaśca tvā sahaśca śrīṇī
tāmāyuśca tvā jarā ca śrīṇītāmātmā ca tvā tanūśca śrīṇītāṃ śṛto'si śṛtaṃkṛtaḥ śṛtāya tvā śṛtebhyastvetyāgnīdhrīye'dhiśrityāha hotarvadasva yatte vādyamiti 3
yadāsya vijānāti yadi śrāto juhotana yadyaśrāto mamattanetyetasminkāle śrātaṃ haviriti pratyuktvā tamādāyāhavanīyaṃ gatvāśrāvya pratyāśrāvite saṃpreṣyati 4
iti tṛtīyā kaṇḍikā

dadhigharmasya yajeti 1
yamindramāhurvaruṇaṃ yamāhuryaṃ mitramāhuryamu satyamāhuḥ | yo devānāṃ devatamastapojāstasmā indrāya sutamājuhomi svāheti vaṣaṭkṛte juhoti | svāhā vaḍindrāyetyanuvaṣaṭkṛte hutvā harati bhakṣam 2
taṃ bhakṣayanti ye pravargyam 3
tasyāraṇye'nuvākyo bhakṣamantraḥ 4
nāpravargye syādityaparam 5
mitro janānpra sa mitreti bhakṣayitvā nābhideśānabhimṛśante 6
vyākhyātā savanīyacaryā 7
etāvannānā | mādhyaṃdinasya savanasyendrāya puroḍāśānāmiti saṃpraiṣādī namati 8
hotra iḍāṃ hṛtvā havirdhānaṃ gacchansaṃpreṣyatyunnīyamānebhyo'nubrūhīti 9
unnayanādyā nārāśaṃsānāṃ sādanāt 10
tatra vikāraḥ 11
acchāvākacamasaṃ daśamamunnayati 12
āśrāvya pratyāśrāvite saṃpreṣyati 13
mādhyaṃdinasya savanasya viṣkevalyasya bhāgasya śukravato manthivato madhuścuta indrāya somāniti saṃpraiṣādiḥ 14
ṣaḍḍhotrā bhavanti 15
purasṭdāgnīdhracamasādacchāvākacamasena caranti 16
etā eva hotrāstṛtīyasavane bhavanti 17
iti caturthī kaṇḍikā iti prathamaḥ paṭalaḥ

sanneṣu nārāśaṃseṣu dakṣiṇā dadāti | bahvaparimitaṃ saptaikaviṃśatiḥ ṣaṣṭiḥ śatam dvādaśaśataṃ sahasraṃ sarvavedasaṃ vā 1
jyeṣṭhaṃ vā putramapabhajya sarvavedasaṃ dadāti 2
aśvataraṃ sāhasre sarvavedase ca dadāti 3
aviṃ dadātyajāṃ gāmaśvaṃ puruṣaṃ hastinaṃ vāso'no rathamodanaṃ manthaṃ māṣāṃstilānvrīhiyavāngardabhamitya
dhikānyaniyatāni 4
gavāṃ saṃkhyā bhavati 5
dakṣiṇena vedimavasthitāsu dakṣiṇāsūttareṇa havirdhānaṃ gatvottareṇāgnīdhrīyaṃ dhiṣṇiyaṃ parītya pūrvayā dvārā prāgvaṃśaṃ praviśyātra yajamānasyāmātyānāṃ saṃhvayanādyā srugdaṇḍopaniyamanātkṛtvā pracaraṇyā dakṣiṇāni juhoti 6
hiraṇyaṃ prabadhya ghṛte'vadhāyodu tyaṃ citramiti dvābhyāṃ gārhapatye juhoti 7
divaṃ gaccha suvaḥ pateti hiraṇyaṃ hutvodgṛhṇāti 8
ubhayaṃ dhārayamāṇo rūpeṇa vo rūpamabhyaimīti dakṣiṇā abhyaiti 9
atinīya vibhāgameke samāmananti 10
tutho vo viśvavedā vibhajatviti tā yajamānaścaturdhā kṛṣṇājinena vyuttrāsya caturthamadhvaryubhyo vibhajati 11
yāvadadhvaryave dadāti tasyārdhaṃ pratiprasthātre tṛtīyaṃ neṣṭre caturthamunnetre 12
etenetareṣāṃ dānamuktam 13
iti pañcamī kaṇḍikā

hiraṇyaṃ pūrṇapātramupabarhaṇaṃ sārvasūtramityagnīdhe'gre dadāti 1
pratihartre'ntataḥ 2
tathā brahmaṇe dadyādyathānyāṃ dakṣiṇāṃ nānudhyāyet 3
aṅgāni dattvā tenatena yathāliṅgaṃ niṣkrīṇīte yadyāsyansyāt 4
hotarvācaṃ te dadāmi tāṃ te'nena niṣkrīṇāmīti 5
evaṃ brahmaṇe manaḥ | adhvaryave prāṇam | udgātre cakṣuḥ |
hotrakebhyaḥ śrotram | camasādhvaryubhyo'ṅgāni | prasarpakebhyo lomāni sadasyāyātmānam 6
anyatra dakṣiṇābhyaścamasādhvaryuprasarpakasadasyebhyaḥ 7
hiraṇyapāṇiragreṇa gārhapatyaṃ nayati jaghanena sadaḥ | antarāgnīdhraṃ ca sadaśca tā udīcīstīrthenotsṛjati 8
etatte agne rādha iti dakṣiṇātinayanaḥ 9
tathaiva samanvārabdheṣvasamanvārabdheṣu vāgne nayetyāgnīdhrīye juhoti 10
vaneṣu vyantarikṣaṃ tatāneti dvitīyāṃ yadyano ratho vāso'dhīvāso vā dīyate yadi vā dāsyansyāt | prajāpate na tvadetānīti tṛtīyāṃ yadi puruṣo hastī vā dīyate yadi vā dāsyansyāt 11
brāhmaṇamadya rādhyāsamityātreyāya prathamāya hiraṇyaṃ
dadāti | dvitīyāya tṛtīyāya vā 12
tadabhāve ya ārṣeyaḥ saṃhitastasmai dadyāt 13
asmaddātrā devatrā gacchateti nītā anumantrya sada etya vi suvaḥ paśyetyanuvīkṣate yadyatinīya vibhajet 14
antaḥ sadasyāsīnebhya ṛtvigbhyo dadyāt | havirdhāne'dhvaryubhyaḥ 15
ṛtvigbhyo namaskaroti 16
yaṃ yajñamāgacchettaṃ prasarpediti prasarpakāṇāṃ vijñāyate 17
iti ṣaṣṭhī kaṇḍikā

dakṣiṇataḥ sadasyāsīnebhyaḥ prasarpakebhyo dadāti 1
na bahirvedi 2
na yācitaḥ 3
na bhītaḥ 4
na kaṇvakaśyapebhyaḥ 5
nābrāhmaṇāya 6
brāhmaṇāyāpyaviduṣe na deyam | apyabrāhmaṇāya vidyāvide dadyāt | yāṃ sa vidyāṃ veda tāṃ tayāvarunddhe 7
yāṃ śrotriyāya jñātaye vānṛtvije prasṛptāya yāṃ sa vidyāṃ veda tāṃ tayāvarunddhe 8
yāṃ jyeṣṭhāya yayā sa devatayā jyaiṣṭhyaṃ gacchati tāṃ tayāvarunddhe 9
yāmārṣeyāya viduṣe svargaṃ tayā lokamāpnoti 10
yāmanyo dīyamānāṃ na kāmayeta yaṃ dviṣyāttasmai dadyātsahānyena dhanena 11
yatpratinuttāṃ dakṣiṇāṃ goṣu cārayetprati vā gṛhṇīyātsalāvṛkyenaṃ bhūtvā pravlinīyāt 12
yāmadānīyāya dakṣiṇāṃ dadāti tāmasya paśavo'nvapakrāmanti | yadi manyetādānīyāyādāmiti na ma idamupadambhiṣagityetadyajurjapedgāṃ vā dadyādbrāhmaṇāya 13
yadā marutvate'nūktamatha na deyaṃ na pratigṛhyam 14
anūbandhyāvapāyāṃ hutāyāṃ dadyātprati ca gṛhṇīyuḥ 15
nītāsu dakṣiṇāsu cātvāle kṛṣṇaviṣāṇāṃ prāsyati hariṇasya raghuṣyato'dhi śīrṣaṇi bheṣajam | sa kṣetriyaṃ
viṣāṇayā viṣūcīnamanīnaśat | anu tvā hariṇo mṛgaḥ paḍbhiścaturbhirakramīt | viṣāṇe viṣyaitaṃ granthiṃ yadasya gulphitaṃ hṛdi mano yadasya gulphitamityetābhyām 16
yajñapatimṛṣaya enasāhurityāgnīdhrīye pañca vaiśvakarmaṇāni hutvā 17
iti saptamī kaṇḍikā

marutvatīyābhyāṃ pracarataḥ 1
indrāya marutvate'nubrūhīndrāya marutvate preṣyeti saṃpraiṣau sānuvaṣaṭkārāvananuvaṣaṭkārau vā | anyataro vā sānuvaṣaṭkāraḥ | dvidevatyavatsaṃpātau vyavanīyābhakṣitena pātreṇādhvaryustṛtīyaṃ marutvatīyaṃ gṛhṇāti |
marutvām̐ indreti grahaṇasādanau | pratiprasthātā harati bhakṣam | ubhāvadhvaryū
pratibhakṣayataḥ 2
etatpātramādāyaindrāgnavacchastrapratigaro grahanārāśaṃsāśca 3
māhendraṃ śukrapātreṇa gṛhṇāti | mahām̐ indro ya ojaseti grahaṇasādanau 4
māhendrasya stotramupākaroti 5
stute vaiśvadevavacchastrapratigaro grahanārāśaṃsāśca 6
māhendraṃ tvatigrāhyā anuhūyante 7
sahaivādhvaryuṇāgneyaṃ pratiprasthātādatte | aindraṃ neṣṭā | sauryamunnetā 8
agne tejasvinnityāgneyaṃ pratiprasthātā hutvā tejovidasītyanumantrayate | indraujasvinnityaindraṃ neṣṭā hutvaujovidasītyanumantrayate | sūrya bhrājasvinniti sauryamunnetā hutvā suvarvidasītyanumantrayate 9
tānhutvā sadasi pratyaṅmukhā bhakṣayanti mayi medhāmityetaiḥ svaṃsvaṃ yathāliṅgam 10
tathaivokthyavigrahāḥ 11
etāvannānā | indrāya tvendrāya tveti sarvatra grahaṇasādanau saṃnamati 12
ukthaṃ vācītyāha mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanaṃ pratigīrya śastraṃśastraṃ vā | viśve devā maruta iti saṃsthite savana āhutiṃ juhoti 13
tathaiva saṃpraiṣaḥ sarpaṇaṃ ca 14
saṃtiṣṭhate mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanam 15
ityaṣṭamī kaṇḍikā iti dvitīyaḥ paṭalaḥ

ādityārambhaṇaṃ tṛtīyasavanam 1
havirdhānasyobhe dvārau saṃvṛtya vedyāṃ bahujanāyām 2
yadi vāsya bhrātṛvyaḥ prasṛptaḥ syādantarvedi sati gṛhṇīyāt 3
bhrātṛvyayajñe tu gṛhyamāṇa āditye bahirvedi tiṣṭhet 4
ādityapātreṇa ya ādityasthālyāṃ dvidevatyagrahasaṃpātāstebhyaḥ somaṃ gṛhṇāti kadā cana starīrasīti 5
kadā cana prayucchasīti śṛtātaṅkyaṃ dadhi 6
yajño devānāmiti punaḥ somaṃ gṛhītvā vivasva ādityeti tasmingrāvāṇamupāṃśusavanamavadhāya tenainaṃ
mekṣayitvā 7
yā divyā vṛṣṭistayā tvā śrīṇāmīti śṛtātaṅkyena dadhnā payasā vā vṛṣṭikāmasya śrītvā grāvāṇamudgṛhṇāti 8
yadyudgṛhītasya tājagbinduḥ praskandedvarṣukaḥ parjanyaḥ syāt | yadi ciramavarṣukaḥ 9
na sādayati 10
yadi kāmayeta garbhānpaśavaḥ srīvyeyurityudgṛhyādityamavekṣeta 11

ā samudrāditi darbhairācyāvayati 15
iti navamī kaṇḍikā

ādityebhyo'nubrūhi priyebhyaḥ priyadhāmabhyaḥ priyavratebhyo mahasvasarasya patibhya urorantarikṣasyādhyakṣebhya ādityebhyaḥ preṣya priyebhyaḥ priyadhāmabhyaḥ priyavratebhyo mahasvasarasya patibhya urorantarikṣasyādhyakṣebhya iti saṃpraiṣau | ādityebhyo'nubrūhyādityebhyaḥ preṣyeti vā 1
yāste viśvāḥ samidhaḥ santyagna iti darbhānāhavanīye prāsyānyatrekṣamāṇa ādityaṃ juhoti 2
unnambhaya pṛthivīmiti vṛṣṭikāmasya juhuyāt 3
na hutvānvīkṣeta 4
sūdavadādityapātramāyatane sādayitvādābhyāṃśumupāṃśupāvanau yaścopāṃśupātre'ṃśustānṛjīṣe'pisṛjya prātaḥsavanavanmahābhiṣavaḥ 5
ṛjīṣaṃ tvevābhiṣuṇvanti 6
payasyāvarjaṃ savanīyāḥ 7

pūrvayā yajamānaḥ prapadyate 9
pūtabhṛto bila udīcīnadaśaṃ pavitraṃ vitatya tasminyajamānaḥ purastātpratyaṅtiṣṭhansaha patnyāśiramavanayatyasme devāso vapuṣe cikitsateti catasṛbhiḥ 10
grahakāla āgrayaṇameva catasṛbhyo dhārābhyaḥ 11
āgrayaṇādutsicya dvitīyāṃ dhārāṃ karoti | ādityasthālyāstṛtīyām | ādityagrahasaṃpātācaturthom 12
ukthyaścedatrokthyaṃ gṛhṇāti 13
viramati dhāraikadhanānāṃ yathārthamityetadādi mādhyaṃdinavat 14
iti daśamī kaṇḍikā
jāgataḥ panthā ādityā devatāvṛkeṇāparipareṇa pathā svastyādityānaśīyeti sarpaṇe vikāraḥ | āyuṣe hiṅkuru tasyai prastuhi tasyai stuhi tasyai me'varuddhyā iti purastādārbhavātpavamānādyajamāno japati | pañcahotāraṃ saptahotāraṃ vā vyācaṣṭe | āyuvai hiṅkuru tasyai prastuhi tasyai stuhi tasyai me'varuddhyā iti ca | stūyamāne ca pañcahotāraṃ saptahotāraṃ vā japati | madhyamāyāṃ ca
stotrīyāyāṃ tṛtīyamanvāroham | stute'dhvaryuḥ saṃpreṣyatyagnīdagnīnvihara barhi stṛṇīhi puroḍāśām̐ alaṃkuru pratiprasthātaḥ paśau saṃvadasveti 1

śṛtaṃ havī3
śamitarityetadādi pāśukaṃ karma pratipadyata eḍāyāḥ 3

prāśitāyāmiḍāyāṃ savanīyādyā nārāśaṃsānāṃ sādanāt 5

ityekādaśī kaṇḍikā

pracaraṇakāle hotṛcamasamadhvaryurādatte | camasāṃścamasādhvaryavaḥ 1
āśrāvya pratyāśrāvite saṃpreṣyati | tṛtīyasya savanasyarbhumato vibhumataḥ prabhumato vājavataḥ savitṛvato bṛhaspativato viśvadevyāvatastībrām̐ āśīrvata indrāya somāniti saṃpraiṣādiḥ 2
atha camasāñjuhoti 3

iti dvādaśī kaṇḍikā iti tṛtīyaḥ paṭalaḥ

nopastha āsīta | yadupastha āsīta paṇḍakaḥ syāt 1
hotṛcamasamukhyāṃścamasānunnayansarvaṃ rājānamunnīya daśābhiḥ kalaśau mṛṣṭvā nyubjati 2

jvalayanti dhiṣṇiyān 4

bhūtamasi bhūte mā dhā iti pratiprasthātā dhruvamavekṣya dyāvāpṛthivībhyāṃ tvā parigṛhṇāmītyañjalinā parigṛhya viśve tvā devā vaiśvānarāḥ pracyāvayantviti hṛtvā dhruvaṃ dhruveṇeti purastātpratyaṅṅāsīno hotṛcamase dhruvamavanayati 1
purastādukthyasyāvanīyaḥ | madhyato'ntato vā 2
uta no'hirbuÞyaḥ śṛṇotvaja ekapāditi vā vaiśvadevyāmṛci śasyamānāyām 3

hotṛcamasamadhvaryuḥ pratibhakṣayati subhūrasi śreṣṭho raśmīnāṃ priyo devānāṃ saṃsadanīyaḥ | taṃ tvā subhava devā abhisaṃviśantviṣo'si tveṣo'si nṛmṇo'si yahvo'si vrato'si svo'si vāraṇo'si tasya ta iṣasya tveṣasya nṛmṇasya yahvasya vratasya svasya vāraṇasya śūdrasya cāryasya ca bhukṣiṣīyeti 8

paridhiṣu prahṛteṣūnnetā hāriyojanaṃ gṛhṇāti 1

samudraṃ vaḥ prahiṇomi svāṃ yonimapigacchata | ariṣṭā asmākaṃ vīrāḥ santu mā parāseci naḥ svam | acchāyaṃ vo marutaḥ śloka etvacchā viṣṇuṃ niṣiktapāmavobhiḥ | uta prajāyai gṛṇate vayo dhuryūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā na ityantarvedi śeṣānninīya dadhikrāvṇo akāriṣamityāgnīdhre dadhidrapsānbhakṣayanti 1
ubhā kavī yuvānā satyā tā dharmaṇaspatī | satyasya dharmaṇaspate vi sakhyāni sṛjāmaha iti tānūnaptriṇaḥ sakhyāni visṛjante 2
paśuvatpatnīsaṃyājāḥ 3
stīrṇe vede juhvāṃ navagṛhītaṃ gṛhītvā dhātā rātirityantarvedyūrdhvastiṣṭhansaṃtataṃ samaśo nava samiṣṭayajūṃṣi juhoti 4
yaṃ kāmayeta pāpīyānsyādityekaikaṃ tasya juhuyājjihmastiṣṭhan | sruveṇa vā vigrāham 5
idaṃ tṛtīyaṃ savanaṃ kavīnāmiti saṃsthite savana āhutiṃ juhoti 6
atra mekhalāyāḥ kṛṣṇaviṣāṇāyāśca cātvāle prāsanaṃ vājasaneyinaḥ samāmananti māhirbhūrmā pṛdākuriti 7
agninā devena pṛtanā jayāmīti yajamāno jāgatānviṣṇukramānkrāmati 8
sarvebhirdevebhiḥ pṛtanā yajāmyānuṣṭubhena chandasaikaviṃśena stomena vairājena sāmnā vaṣaṭkāreṇa vajreṇa sarvajānbhrātṛvyānadharānpādayāmyavainānbādhe pratyenānnude'sminkṣaye'sminbhūmiloke yo'smāndveṣṭi yaṃ ca vayaṃ dviṣmo viṣṇoḥ krameṇātyenānkrāmāmīti caturthameke samāmananti 9
indreṇa sayujo vayamityāhavanīyaṃ yajamāna upatiṣṭhate 10
ityaṣṭādaśī kaṇḍikā iti pañcamaḥ paṭalaḥ

avabhṛthasya tantraṃ prakramayati 1
vedaṃ kṛtvāgnīnparistīrya pāṇiprakṣālanādi karma pratipadyate | yathārthaṃ pātrayogaḥ 2
nirvapaṇakāle vāruṇamekakapālaṃ nirvapati 3
caturgṛhītānyājyāni vāruṇaṃ cālaṃkṛtyottaravedyaṃsa āsādayati 4
atra yajamāna audumbarīmutkhidatyupasṛjandharuṇaṃ mātre mātarā dharuṇo dhayanniha puṣṭiṃ puṣṭipatirniyacchatu rāyaspoṣamiṣamūrjamasmāsu dīdharaditi 5
tāmadhiṣavaṇacarmaphalake sarvāṇi ca somaliptānyantarā cātvālotkarāvuttare vā vedyaṃsa audumbaryāmāsandyāṃ sādayati | anyatra catasṛbhyaḥ somasthālībhyaḥ 6
ava te heḍo varuṇa namobhiriti yajamānaścātvāle kṛṣṇāśinaṃ prāsyati 7
punarvainena dīkṣeta vasīta vainadbhrastāṃ vainatsrucāmavadhānārthāṃ kārayet | haviravahananārthaṃ vā syāt 8
avabhṛthādudetya putrāya brahmacāriṇe vā dadyādityeke 9
āyurdā agne haviṣo juṣāṇa ityavabhṛthamavaiṣyañjuhuyāt | avabhṛtha nicaṅkuṇeti ca 10
ityekonaviṃśī kaṇḍikā

namo rudrāya vāstoṣpataya āyane vidravaṇa udyāne yatparāyaṇa āvartane vivartane yo gopāyati taṃ huva iti ca 1
uruṃ hi rājā varuṇaścakāreti vedyā bhiprayānto vadanti | cātvālādvā 2
prastotaḥ sāma gāyeti 3
sarve sahapatnīkāstriḥ sāmno nidhanamupayanti | ardhādhve dvitīyam | prāpya tṛtīyam 4
sarvatra saṃpreṣyati 5
sarvā diśo'vabhṛthagamanamāmnātamityetadādyā caryā yāḥ 6
niṣkāsavadvāruṇena pracaryāpabarhiṣāvanūyājau yajati | na vā 7
yatte grāvṇāpyāyasva saṃ ta iti saumībhirdrapsavatībhiḥ pañcabhiḥ saptabhistrayodaśabhirvā dadhnaudumbaraśākhayarjīṣaṃ prokṣati 8
prahṛtya vābhijuhuyāt 9
ṛjīṣasya srucaṃ pūrayitvāpsūpamārayati samudre te hṛdayamapsvantariti 10
tato yo bhindurutsravate tamupaspṛśedbhakṣayedvāpsu dhautasya soma deva ta iti 11
samudraṃ vaḥ prahiṇomīti sarvāṇi ca somaliptānyavabhṛthe pravidhyati 12
vicṛtto varuṇasya pāśa iti yajamāno mekhalāṃ vicacṛte | imaṃ viṣyāmīti patnī yoktam 13
atra yoktamekhale vāsasī jālaṃ kṛṣṇājinaṃ cāvabhṛthe pravidhya 14
iti viṃśī kaṇḍikā

devīrāpa ityavabhṛthaṃ yajamāno'bhimantrya sumitrā na āpa oṣadhaya ityapaḥ pragāhya saśiraskāvanupamakṣantau snātaḥ patnī yajamānaśca 1
anyo'nyasya pṛṣṭhe pradhāvataḥ 2
yaddidīkṣe manasā yacca vācā yadvā prāṇaiścakṣuṣā yacca śrotreṇa | yadretasā mithunenāpyātmanādbhyo lokā dadhire teja indriyam | śukrā dīkṣāyai tapaso vimocanīrāpo bimoktīrmayi teja indriyam | yadṛcā sāmnā yajuṣā
paśūnāṃ carmanhaviṣā didīkṣe | yacchandobhiroṣadhībhirvanaspatāvadbhyo lokā dadhire teja indriyam | śukrā dīkṣāyai tapaso vimocanīrāpo vimoktrīrmayi teja indriyam | yena brahma yena kṣatraṃ yenendrāgnī prajāpatiḥ somo varuṇo yena rājā | viśve devā ṛṣayo yena prāṇā adbhyo lokā dadhire teja indriyam | śukrā dīkṣāyai tapaso vimocanīrāpo vimoktrīrmayi teja indriyamiti trirañjalinā viṣicyonnetarvasīyo na unnayābhi | uditte vasuvittamā giraḥ stomāsa īrate | satrājito dhanasā akṣitotayo vājayanto rathā iva | kaṇvā iva bhṛgavaḥ sūryā iva viśvamiddhītamānaśuriti yajamānaḥ saṃpreṣyati 3
ityekaviṃśī kaṇḍikā

udeta prajāmāyurvarco dadhānā adha syāmasurubhayorgṛheṣu | gāyatrīṃ chandāṃsyanusaṃrabhantāmasmānrāya uta yajñāḥ sacantām | suprītaḥ suvarapa āviveśetyunnetonnayati 1
ahate vasānāvuditaḥ 2
somoṣṇīṣaṃ yajamānaḥ paridhatte | somopanahanaṃ patnī somapariśrayaṇaṃ vā 3
te udavasānīyāyāmadhvaryave dattaḥ 4
udvayaṃ tamasasparītyādityamupasthāya pratiyuto varuṇasya pāśa ityudakāntaṃ pratyasitvā samitpāṇaya unnetāraṃ puraskṛtyāpratīkṣamāyantyapāma somamiti mahīyāṃ vadanto yānyapāmityānyapratīttānyasmi yamasya balinā carāmi | ihaiva santaḥ prati tadyātayāmo jīvā jīvebhyo niharāma enat | anṛṇā asminnanṛṇāḥ parasmiṃstṛtīye loke anṛṇāḥ syāma | ye devayānā uta pitṛyāṇāḥ sarvānpatho anṛṇā ā kṣīyemeti ca 5
edho'syedhiṣīmahītyāhavanīye samidha ādhāyāpo anvacāriṣamityupatiṣṭhante 6
iti dvāviṃśī kaṇḍikā iti ṣaṣṭhaḥ paṭalaḥ

prāyaṇīyāvadudayanīyā 1
tasyāmeva sthālyāmaniṣkasitāyāṃ śrapayati | tadbarhiḥ | tanmekṣaṇam 2
śālāmukhīye pracaranti 3
teṣveva deśeṣvagnimājyabhāgānāṃ prathamaṃ yajati | pathyāṃ svastimuttamām 4
yāḥ prāyaṇīyasya yājyā ityuktam 5
maitrāvaruṇīṃ gāṃ vaśāmanūbandhyāmālabhate 6
tasyā nirūḍhapaśubandhavatkalpaḥ 7
imtrāvaruṇābhyāṃ gorvapāyā medaso'nubrūhi mitrā
varuṇābhyāṃ gorvapāyā medasaḥ preṣyeti saṃpraiṣau 8
evamavadāneṣu haviṣa ityantau namati 9
tisru'nūbandhyā eke samāmananti 10
maitrāvaruṇīṃ vaiśvadevīṃ bārhaspatyāmiti 11
dvirūpā maitrāvaruṇī | bahurūpā vaiśvadevī | rohiṇī
bārhaspatyā 12
upāṃśu vaiśvadevyā madhyataścaranti 13
tā na sarvatrālabheta vājapeye rājasūye sattre sahasre sarvavedase vā 14
yaḥ kāmayeta sarvo me yajñaḥ syātsarasa iti sa etāstisro'nūbandhyā ālabheta 15
anūbandhyāvapāyāṃ hutāyāṃ dakṣiṇe vedyante yajamānaḥ keśaśmaśru vāpayate 16
anūbandhyāyāḥ paśupuroḍāśam 17
iti trayoviṃśī kaṇḍikā

devikāhavīṃṣi nirvapati 1
dhātre puroḍāśaṃ dvādaśakapālamiti pañca 2
yāsu sthālīṣu somā bhavanti tāsūttarāṇi catvāri havīṃṣi śrapayati 3
samānaṃ tu sviṣṭakṛdiḍam 4
devikā nirvapetprajākāma iti kāmyāḥ 5
anūbandhyāyāṃ svaruṃ juhoti | hṛdayaśūlamudvāsayati 6
viṣṇukramānkrāmati 7
rājño rājaṃbhavyasya vānūbandhyāyāḥ paśupuroḍāśamaṣṭau devasuvāṃ havīṃṣyanunirvapatyagnaye gṛhapataya iti 8
samānaṃ tu sviṣṭakṛdiḍam 9
maitrāvaruṇīmāmikṣāmanūbandhyāyāḥ sthāne bahvṛcāḥ samāmananti | tasyā agreṇa havirdhānamāsīno'navānaḥ hotā yajati | havirāhutiprabhṛtīḍāntā saṃtiṣṭhate | prayājaprabhṛtyājyabhāgaprabhṛti vā | iḍāntāmeke samāmananti 10
kṛtsnasaṃsthāmeke 11
tāmanu devikāhavīṃṣi nirvapati 12
samānaṃ tu sviṣṭakṛdiḍam 13
sadaso havirdhānasya havirdhānayoriti prathamagrathitāngranthīnvisrasyodīcī havirdhāne bahirvedi nirvartayati 14
āhavanīyādulmukamādāya yajamāno vedimupoṣati yatkusīdamapratīttamiti 15
yadi miśramiva caredañjalinā saktūnpradāvye juhuyādviśvalopa viśvadāvasya tveti 16
yadākūtāditi tisṛbhirdhūmamanumantrayate 17
ahnāṃ vidhānyāmityuktam 18
ayaṃ no nabhasā pura ityetairyathābrāhmaṇamupasthāya 19
iti caturviṃśī kaṇḍikā

vedamupastha ādhāyāntarvedyāsīno'tīmokṣāñjapati 1
atra viṣṇukramāneke samāmananti 2
prājahitaṃ samāropya śālāmukhīyaṃ dvitīyaṃ gataśriyaḥ prāṅdaṅvodavasāyedamū nu śreyo'vasānamāganma śive no dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe ime | gomaddhanavadaśvavadūrjasvatsuvīrā vīrairanusaṃcaremeti devayajanamadhyavasāya nirma
nthyodavasānīyāyāstantraṃ prakramayati 3
agnīnanvādhāya vedaṃ kṛtvāgnīnparistīrya pāṇiprakṣālanādi karma pratipadyate | yathārthaṃ pātrayogaḥ 4
nirvapaṇakāla āgneyaṃ pañcakapālaṃ nirvapati | aṣṭākapālaṃ vā | yadi pañcakapālo gāyatryau saṃyājye | yadyaṣṭākapālaḥ paṅktyau 5
anaḍvāndakṣiṇā | anaḍudarhaṃ vā hiraṇyam 6
siddhamiṣṭiḥ saṃtiṣṭhate | vaiṣṇavīṃ pūrṇāhutimudavasānīyāyāḥ sthāne vājasaneyinaḥ samāmananti 7
dvādaśagṛhītena srucaṃ pūrayitvedaṃ viṣṇurvicakrama ityantarvedyūrdhvastiṣṭhañjuhoti 8
sā yāvadrātreṣṭiḥ saṃtiṣṭhate'tha sāyamagnihotraṃ juhoti | kāle prātarhomam 9
saṃtiṣṭhate'gniṣṭomo'gniṣṭomaḥ 10
iti pañcaviṃśī kaṇḍikā iti saptamaḥ paṭalaḥ
iti trayodaśaḥ praśnaḥ

ukthyaḥ ṣoḍaśyatirātro'ptoryāmaścāgniṣṭomasya guṇavikārāḥ 1
ukthyena paśukāmo yajeta | ṣoḍaśinā vīryakāmaḥ | atirātreṇa prajākāmaḥ paśukāmo vā | aptoryāmeṇātirātreṇa sarvānkāmānavāpnoti 2
teṣāmagniṣṭomavatkalpaḥ 3
uktāśca vikārāḥ 4
yathā sadaḥ kratukaraṇaṃ kratupaśavaḥ ṣoḍaśinaḥ somakrayaṇī pātramiti 5
ukthyaścedagniṣṭomacamasānunnayaṃstribhyaścamasagaṇebhyo rājānamatirecayati 6
agnīnneṣṭurupasthamāsīda neṣṭaḥ patnīmudānayonnetarhotuścamasamanūnnaya hotṛcamase dhruvāyāvakāśaṃ kurūdgātrā patnīṃ saṃkhyāpayāpa upapravartayeti saṃpraiṣaḥ 7
agniṣṭomacamasaiḥ pracarya prātaḥsavanavadukthyaṃ vigṛhṇāti 8
etāvannānā | indrāvaruṇābhyāṃ tveti prathame grahaṇasādanau saṃnamati | indrābṛhaspatibhyāṃ tveti dvitīye | indrāviṣṇubhyāṃ tveti tṛtīye 9
unnetaḥ sarvaṃ rājānamunnaya mātirīrico daśābhiḥ kalaśau mṛṣṭvā nyubjeti sarvasaṃsthāsūttame gaṇa etatsaṃpreṣyati 10
iti prathamā kaṇḍikā

agnīdaupayajānaṅgārānāharetyetadādyāgniṣṭomikaṃ karma sarvasaṃsthāsu samānam 1
yadyu vai ṣoḍaśyukthyacamasānāmuttamaṃ gaṇamunnayannekasmai camasagaṇāya rājānamatirecayati 2
ṣoḍaśino grahaṇam 3
prātaḥsavana uttamo dhārāgrahāṇām 4
savanesavane vā 5
athaikeṣām | pūrvayoḥ savanayoḥ purastāduttamādukthyaparyāyādukthyādgṛhṇīyātsarvaiḥ pracarite | tṛtīyasavana āgrayaṇāt 6
api vā tṛtīyasavana evāgrayaṇātpaśukāmasya 7
nokthye gṛhṇīyāt | gṛhṇīyādvā 8
atirātre paśukāmasya | atirātre brahmavarcasakāmasya 9
apyagniṣṭome rājanyasya gṛhṇīyāt 10
sastutaśastro bhavati 11
ātiṣṭha vṛtrahanniti grahaṇasādanau 12
yasmānna jātaḥ paro anyo asti ya āviveśa bhuvanāni viśvā | prajāpatiḥ prajayā saṃvidānastrīṇi jyotīṃṣi sacate sa ṣoḍaśī | eṣa brahmā ya ṛtviyaḥ | indro nāma śruto gaṇe | pra te mahe vidathe śaṃsiṣaṃ harī | ya ṛtviyaḥ pra te vanve | vanuṣo haryataṃ madam | indro nāma ghṛtaṃ na yaḥ | haribhiścāru secate | śruto gaṇa ā tvā viśantu | harivarpasaṃ gira ityetābhiścatasṛbhiḥ sannamabhimantrya 13
iti dvitīyā kaṇḍikā

samayāviṣite sūrye hiraṇyena ṣoḍaśinaḥ stotramupākaroti 1
barhiḥsthāne bhavati 2
śvetamaśvaṃ purastāddhārayanti | aruṇapiśaṅgaṃ vā 3
othā moda iva made madā moda ivomatheti vyatiṣakta ubhayatomodaḥ pratigara ānuṣṭugbhyaḥ 4
indrādhipate'dhipatistvaṃ devānāmasyadhipatiṃ māmāyuṣmantaṃ varcasvantaṃ manuṣyeṣu kurviti hutvendraśca samrāḍvaruṇaśca rājā tau te bhakṣaṃ cakraturagra etam | tayoranu bhakṣaṃ bhakṣayāmi vāgjuṣāṇā somasya tṛpyatviti ṣoḍaśinaṃ bhakṣayati | ādityavadgaṇena camasān 5
anuṣṭup chandasa iti bhakṣamantraṃ namati 6
aruṇapiśaṅgo'śvo dakṣiṇā | aśvatarī vā 7
atirātraścetṣoḍaśicamasānunnayaṃstrayodaśabhyaścamasagaṇebhyo rājānamatirecayati 8
ṣoḍaśinā pracarya rātriparyāyaiḥ pracarati 9
hotṛcamasamukhyaḥ prathamo gaṇaḥ | maitrāvaruṇacamamukhyo dvitīyaḥ | brāhmaṇācchaṃsicamasamukhyastṛtīyaḥ | acchāvākacamasamukhyaścaturthaḥ 10
indrāya tvāpiśarvarāyeti mukhyaṃmukhyaṃ camasamanūnnayati 11
sarvaindrī rātriḥ 12
anuṣṭup chandasa iti sarvatra bhakṣamantraṃ namati 13
prathamābhyāṃ gaṇābhyāmadhvaryuścarati | uttarābhyāṃ pratiprasthātā 14
eṣa prathamaḥ paryāyaḥ 15
evaṃ vihito dvitīyastṛtīyaśca 16
iti tṛtīyā kaṇḍikā

atha pratiprasthātāśvinaṃ dvikapālaṃ nirvapati 1
hotṛcamasamukhyānsaṃdhicamasānunnayati 2
trivṛdrāthaṃtaraḥ saṃdhiḥ 3
āśvinaṃ paraḥsahasraṃ śastraṃ bhavati 4
tadudita āditye paridhīyate 5
tasminparidhite hotṛcamasamadhvaryurādatte | camasāṃścamasādhvaryavaḥ | puroḍāśaṃ pratiprasthātā 6
aśvibhyāṃ tiroahniyānāṃ somānāmanubrūhyaśvibhyāṃ tiroahniyānāṃ somānāṃ preṣyeti saṃpraiṣau 7
tiroahniyānsomānprasthitānpreṣyeti vā 8
na vā prasthāpayet 9
saha somaiḥ puroḍāśaṃ pratiprasthātā sarvahutaṃ juhoti 10

iti caturthī kaṇḍikā iti prathamaḥ paṭalaḥ

kratupaśava aikādaśināśca vikalpante 1
teṣāṃ samavāye yathācoditaṃ saṃskārāḥ 2
tantramaṅgāni vibhavanti 3
pratyakṣārthāni pratisaṃskāramadhyāvartante 4
yathārthamūhaḥ 5

sarveṣāṃ śakalāharaṇāvraścanahomāḥ 7
